##### **FIGURE 3.1**

Jay Wheelock. Animated Portrait. Zoetrope components ki teen photos. 2010. Photo (a) wo gol (circular) base hai jahan 3D-printed heads rakhe gaye hain. Photo (b) heads mein se ek ka rendering hai. Photo (c) 3D-printed heads ka ek collection hai, jinhe circular base par rakha jana hai.

Baad mein, unhe apni thesis ke liye ek dusra idea aaya: chehre ki muscles (facial musculature) ki research studies par aadharit rigging points—computer animation ka ek technical pehlu—design karna. Unhone Paul Ekman jaise psychologists ko dhoondha jinhone emotion aur expression ki study ki thi, aur unhone muscles par ek aur elective class li jo unke major se bahar thi. Wo dang (fascinated) thin, lekin wo yeh nahi samajh paa rahi thin ki ek artist ke taur par wo is research mein kya jod (add) sakti hain. Thoda aur kaam karne ke baad, unhone animated portraits ke sath experiment karne ka faisla kiya. Unhone aise digital artists ko dhoondha jinhone is tarah ka kaam kiya tha, aur unhe pata chala ki kisi aur ne abhi tak aisa nahi kiya hai. Yeh unki MFA thesis ban gayi: "animated portraits" jo kisi insaan ki personality ko darshate (depict) hain.

Wheelock ki kahani pichle do chapters ke kuch bade sawalon ka jawab deti hai: Jab aap nahi jante ki aap kahan ja rahe hain toh aap kaam karna kaise jaari rakhte hain? Bina kisi manzil (destination) ke aap kisi iradatan (intentional) kaam mein kaise involve (engage) hote hain? Wo shuru se hi janti thin ki unhe insaani chehre ko model karne mein dilchaspi hai. Lekin zoetrope aur animated portraits apne program se bahar do elective classes lene par an-soche dhang se samne (emerged) aaye. Aap jante hain ki yeh ek jhaadi (bush) hai, lekin aap nahi jante ki yeh kis tarah ki jhaadi hai. Kaam karne ka process idea ke samne aane (emergence) ka raasta banta hai.

Zyada-tar jobs mein, hamein pata hota hai ki hum kya karne ki koshish kar rahe hain. Hamein pata hota hai ki aakhiri point (endpoint) kya hai; hamein pata hota hai ki jab hum khatam karenge toh duniya kaisi hogi. Har pal (moment), hamein is baat ka andaza hota hai ki hum aakhir ke kitne kareeb hain aur hum waqt par (deadline) kaam poora kar payenge ya nahi. Hamein pata hota hai ki hum har din kya kar rahe hain aur wo roz ka kaam hamein goal ke aur kareeb aane mein kaise madad karta hai.

Lekin professional artists aur designers ke liye creativity is tarah kaam nahi karti. Creativity idea se lekar use poora karne (realization) tak ka ek aasan rasta nahi hai. Jab Wheelock ne apni thesis par shuruat ki, toh unhe is baat ka koi saaf idea nahi tha ki yeh kahan ja rahi hai. Unke paas bas kuch dhundhle (vague) ehsaas, sambhavnaon ki kuch jhalak (wisps of possibilities) thin. Kuch toh tha, kuch aisa jo unhe dhoondhna tha. Lekin jhaadi (bush) ko kaise dhoondhein? Aapko vishwas (trust) karna hoga ki yeh process se khud ubhar kar samne (emerge) aayegi.

Is process mein lagatar nayi cheezein try karna shamil hai—images, techniques, ya ideas—bina kisi saaf irade (explicit intention) ya plan ke, aur bina yeh jane ki iska nateeja (turn out) kya hoga. Yeh experiments utni hi baar band raston (dead ends) ki taraf le jate hain jitni baar wo aage explore karne layaq umeed-bhare (promising) kaam ki taraf le jate hain. Failures (nakaamiyan) bhi process se waise hi ubhar kar aati hain, bilkul successes (kamyabiyon) ki tarah. Jab tak yeh ho nahi jata, aap nahi jante ki yeh kaun sa hoga. Yahi wajah hai ki artists aur designers failure ki umeed karte hain aur yahan tak ki failure ka swagat (welcome) bhi karte hain, jaisa ki hum agle chapter mein seekhenge.

Wheelock un das (10) MFA students mein se ek hain jinka maine interview liya. Unme se zyada-tar pehle hi chaar-saal ka undergraduate BFA program poora kar chuke the. Misaal ke taur par, Wheelock ke paas pehle se hi SCAD se animation mein BFA tha. Expertise (maharat) ke is level par, MFA students jante hain ki ek aise process mein kaise shamil hona hai jo is baat ke saaf idea ke bina shuru hota hai ki yeh kahan jayega. Wo jante hain ki unke sabhi experiments aise poore (finished) kaam tak nahi pahunchenge jinhe publicly display kiya ja sake. Wo mistakes, dead ends, iterations (dohraav), aur disha (direction) mein badlaav aane ki umeed (anticipate) rakhte hain. Jab main unke studios mein unka interview le raha tha, toh unme se kaiyon ne mujhe aise kaam dikhaye—jo abhi bhi deewaron par ya shelves par rakhe the—jin par unhone kaam karna band kar diya tha aur jinhe unka publicly display karne ka koi plan nahi tha. Wo in adhoore (unfinished) kaamon, in adhoore band raston se sharminda (ashamed) nahi the. Asal mein, unhe in par garv (proud) lag raha tha; wo in par hase, unhe inke liye ek dostana pyaar (friendly love) tha. Unhone apni failures ko sambhal kar rakha tha. Artists aur designers kabhi cheezon ko phenkte (throw away) nahi hain. Har dead end waqti (temporary) hota hai; yeh baad mein ek mumkin future advance (aage badhne ka rasta) mein badal sakta hai.

Aksar ek failed idea baad mein kaam aata hai (useful), jab creative rasta aage khulta hai (unfolds), aur artist naye kaam ke sandarbh (context) mein us idea ka naya matlab (reinterprets) nikalta hai. Pichle chapter mein, humne ek aur MFA student, Jennifer Baker ke baare mein padha tha, aur yeh padha tha ki unki motherhood (maatritva) ki theme doodh (milk) ke sath experimentations se kaise ubhar kar samne (emerged) aayi thi. Lekin doodh akela material nahi tha jiske sath wo experiment kar rahi thin. Unhone mujhe bataya: "Aese bahut se kaam hain jo maine shuru kiye hain jinhe kisi ne kabhi nahi dekha." Baker ne ubharne (emergence) ke ek bhatakne wale (wandering) process ko bayan (describe) kiya:

*Mujhe samajh nahi aa raha tha ki main jo bana rahi thi wo kyun bana rahi thi. Main kuch banane ke liye majboor (compelled) hoon aur mujhe poori tarah se nahi pata ki yeh kis baare mein hai. Art banana aur creativity isi ko dhoondhne (figuring this out) ke baare mein hai. Mere kaam ne kai baar disha (direction) badli. Mera process kaam ke dauran ek an-soche (unpredictable) dhang se evolve (viksit) hua aur ubharkar samne (emerged) aaya. Aap cheezon ke sath ched-chad karke (messing around) creativity seekhte hain. Aap jab tak iske sath khel na lein, aap uske baare mein baat nahi kar sakte jo aap kar rahe hain. Sabse pehle, mujhe is baat se wakif (acquainted) hone ki zaroorat hai ki main kya kar rahi hoon.*

Baker ne mujhe un projects ki ek lambi list ke baare mein bataya jin par wo kaam kar rahi thin. Inmein se sirf kuch hi unke thesis show mein the:

* Wo ek collaborative project mein shamil hain jahan wo mitti (dirt) mein gaddha (hole) khodte hue khud ka ek video banati hain jabki ek collaborator cake banata hai, aur phir wo cake batter aur gaddhe ki mitti ka istemal karke paper banate hain.
* Wo ek mare huye hiran ke bacche (dead fawn) ko nehlane (bathing) ka apna ek video banana chahti thin, jisme pani tapakne (dripping) ki aawaz ho, jo nehlane ki nishandahi (signify) kare (dekhein figure 3.2). Hamare interview mein, unhone mujhe bataya ki unhone apna man badal liya tha aur wo video ke bajaye photos banane wali thin. Lekin, apne thesis show mein, unke paas asal mein ek hiran ke bache ko dhoye jane ka video tha.
* Unhe poora yakeen hai ki unki thesis mein ghode ke dhar (horse's torso) ki ek abstract casting shamil hogi.
* Wo milk paintings banane ke liye khud ko doodh tapkate (dripping milk) hue videotape karne wali thin, lekin unhone ise aage nahi badhaya (didn't follow through).
* Unhone paper banaya, aur jab yeh geela tha, toh unhone ise ek object par rakh diya taaki sookhne par ise aakar (shape) diya ja sake. Unhone is paper sculpture ko sookhne ke baad tel (oiling) lagane ki koshish ki. Isse butter aur wax (mom) se seb (apples) cast karne ka idea aaya.

Kya yeh dead ends "failures (nakaamiyan)" thin? Artists is tarah nahi sochte. Wo iske bajaye is baare mein sochte hain ki kya process ke ek productive (faydemand) tareeqe se aage badhne ki sambhavna hai. Art aur design ek iterative, an-soche (unpredictable), improvisational (mauqe par banane wale) process se ubhar kar (emerge) aate hain. Jazz ki tarah, aap nahi jante ki aap kya bajane wale hain jab tak ki aap bajana shuru na kar dein. Ideas aapke piano ya aapke saxophone se ubharte hain; wo aapko hairan (surprise) kar dete hain. Agar aap khali music paper ke samne pencil hath mein lekar baithe hote, toh aapko wo ideas nahi aate. Aapko wo ideas *aa hi nahi sakte the*. Wo mauqe ke hisab se liye gaye action (improvised action) ki creativity se ubharkar samne aaye.

**FIGURE 3.2** Jennifer Baker ka studio, Washington University in St. Louis. 2011.

### **Bina Jaane Kaam Karna (Acting without Knowing)**

Jab aapko nahi pata hota ki aap kya kar rahe hain toh shuruat karna mushkil hota hai. Aap ek khali canvas ya sketchbook ke page ko ghoor rahe hote hain. Shuruaat karne wale undergraduates ke liye, yeh zaroori hai ki professors unhe kisi tarah ka anchor point (shuruati point) dein. Pichle chapter mein, humne kai professors ko yeh kehte hue suna tha ki aapko materials ke sath kaam karna shuru karna hoga. Isse koi farq nahi padta ki object kya hai; koi bhi object chalega. Pichla chapter sculpture ke students jo phas jate (stuck) hain, unke liye Steve DeFrank ki advice ki details ke sath khatam hua tha: "Jao ek dabba (box) banao." Lekin dabba sirf ek anchor point hai, kuch aisa jise jaldi hi bhula diya jayega. Aap kuch kar rahe hain, aap ek process mein shamil (engaged) hain, lekin aap abhi tak nahi jante ki aap kahan ja rahe hain. Ek advanced student ke paas process ka bahut tajurba (experience) hota hai aur use yakeen hota hai ki, aakhir-kaar, kuch na kuch ubharkar aayega (emerge). Shuruaat karne wale students abhi yeh nahi jante, aur wo is early stage mein bahut bechain (anxious) ho sakte hain. Yahi wajah hai ki unhe ek assignment ke structure ki zaroorat hoti hai—professor dwara di gayi constraints (rukawaton) ka ek set (dekhein chapter 6, "Constraints").

Lekin aap bina manzil ke (aimlessly) aage nahi badhte. Aapka process motivated (prerit) hota hai, kisi na kisi cheez se chalaya jata (driven) hai—ek dhundhla (vague) ehsaas; ek aabhas (intuition). Creative process ek paradox (virodhabhas/uljhan) hai. Wheelock ki tarah, aap jante hain ki ek jhaadi (bush) hai, lekin aap abhi tak nahi jante ki yeh kya hai. Aapka vision poori tarah badal sakta hai. Lekin aap is ehsaas se chaltay hain ki kya hone ki zaroorat hai. Baker doodh (milk) se dang (fascinated) thin aur unhe lagta tha ki isey explore karna banta hai, bhale hi unhe nahi pata tha ki kyun aur unhe nahi pata tha ki yeh kahan ja kar khatam hoga. Creative process soche-samjhe irade (deliberateness) aur bina manzil ke bhatakne (aimlessness) ke beech, narrow focus (sikudi hui soch) aur khuli sambhavnaon (open possibilities) ke beech ek kashmakash (tension) hai. Creating (banane ka amal) uljhan (ambiguity) aur yakeen (certainty) ke beech ki jagah mein hota hai. Art aur design school aapko is jagah mein phalna-phoolna (thrive) sikhata hai.

Philosophers ne structure aur improvisation ke beech ki is kashmakash (tension) ko dashko (decades) tak study kiya hai—yeh insaani kamo (human action) ko samjhane ki problem hai. Hum har din jo kuch bhi karte hain, usme se zyada-tar ka ek goal (maqsad) hota hai. Kuch kaam rate-rataye (rote) aur dohrane wale (repetitive) hote hain, jaise tyre badalna ya apne coffee mein cream ki utni hi matra aur usi cereal ke bowl ke sath apna aam breakfast taiyar karna. Lekin aaj ki is pechida (complex) duniya mein kai kaamon ke liye, hamein theek se nahi pata hota ki apna goal kaise haasil karna hai; hamein theek se nahi pata hota ki task ko kaise poora karna hai. Agar aap apne field mein tajurbe (experience) wale professional hain—chahe engineering ho, teaching ho, law ho ya social work—aapne aise kamo (action) mein shamil hona seekh liya hai jo structure aur improvisation (mauqe par badlav) ke beech balance (santulan) banate hain. Ek professional hone ka yahi matlab hai. Art aur design ke professions ke sath bhi yahi hai—aap uljhan (ambiguity) aur sambhavna (possibility) ki jagah mein operate karna seekhte hain. BFA ya MFA degree hasil karne wale students ke liye, art sirf ek shauk (calling) nahi hai; yeh ek profession hai.

Ek intention (irada) hone ka kya matlab hai, jab aapke kaam ka nateeja (endpoint) aksar aapke shuruati plan se itna alag hota hai? Aap ek aane wale kal (future state) ki taraf ek powerful vision kaise rakh sakte hain, aur sath hi un an-sochi tabdeelion (unexpected developments) ke liye bhi khule reh sakte hain jinke liye improvisation ki zaroorat hoti hai? Philosophers *intentionality (irade)* ke concept ke zariye human action ki problem ko explore karte hain. Human action ki sabse aasan theory yeh hai ki yeh ek intention (irade) se shuru hoti hai, aur phir aisa kaam (action) kiya jata hai jo us intention ko poora karta hai. Lekin yeh action ka ek linear model hai aur yeh sirf un sthir (stable) situations mein kaam karta hai jahan improvisation ki zaroorat nahi hoti. Yeh model art ya design ke iterative process ko nahi samjha sakta. Artists aur designers dwara sikhaya jane wala creative process jazz improvisation jaisa hai—bina irade ka musical action.

Yeh creativity ka paradox (uljhan) hai: Aapko kisi cheez ke sath shuruat karni hoti hai, aur phir bhi usse bahut zyada attach (juda hua) nahi hona hota. Isey "intention (irada)" kehna sahi nahi hai kyunki yeh ek goal (maqsad) ki taraf ishara karta hai. Endpoint par focus karna, ki aap kahan ja rahe hain, linear insight wala nazariya hai jo shuruaat karne wale students ka hota hai; acha kaam is tarah generate nahi hota. Ek successful creative process mein, aap ek anchor point, ek aazmaishi (trial) idea ke sath shuruat karte hain. Raste mein, aapka irada (intention) aksar badalta rehta hai. Aap usse alag cheez par khatam karte hain jisse aapne shuruat ki thi. Aap yeh jane bina kaam karte hain ki aap kya kar rahe hain. Process samne khulta hai (unfolds) aur baad mein hi aapko ehsaas hota hai ki aapne kya kiya hai.

Art aur design materials ke baare mein nahi hain, bhale hi materials zaroori (essential) hon. Art aur design ideas ke baare mein nahi hain, bhale hi ideas raste mein har jagah hote hon. Art aur design process ke baare mein hain. Ideas, irada (intention), aur meaning (matlab) baad mein (retrospectively) samne aate hain—kaam ke dauran aur kaam khatam hone ke baad.

### **Nayi Problems Dhoondhna (Finding New Problems)**

**Designers problems ko solve nahi karte. Wo problem spaces (problem ki jagahon) mein raasta nikalte (negotiate) hain.**

Allan Chochinov, industrial design, School of Visual Arts

Students ache problem solvers banna seekh rahe hain, lekin yeh wording (shabd) poori tarah theek nahi hai. Haan, artists aur designers problems ko solve karte hain, lekin aakhir-kaar, wo kuch bahut zyada dilchasp (intriguing), kuch aisa karte hain jise pakadna (pin down) kahin zyada mushkil hai: wo *nayi problems dhoondhte* hain.

Amy Honchell, ek painter aur sculptor jo School of the Art Institute of Chicago (SAIC) mein padhati hain, unhone kaha: "Creative problem solving number-ek skill hai jo art school, design schools mein students seekhte hain. Yeh ek aisi skill hai jo kai contexts (halaaton) mein kaam aati hai, na ki sirf art aur design ke context mein. Yeh bas duniya ko dekhne (approach) ka aapka tareeqa ban jata hai." Lekin kuch hi minute baad, wo saaf (clarifies) karti hain: yeh sach mein nayi problems dhoondhne ke baare mein hai. "Problem solve karne ke alawa, yeh problem ko pehchan-na (identifying) bhi hai. Aap is tareeqe se dekh rahe hote hain jo sawal poochta hai, bajaye iske ki sirf sawalon ke jawab dhoondhein. Pehle din se hi yahan ki education ka core (buniyad) yahi hai."

Louise Sandhaus, California Institute of the Arts (CalArts) mein ek graphic designer, students ko "possibility makers (sambhavnayein banane wale)" banna sikha rahi hain. Jab maine unhe suggest kiya ki designers problem solvers hote hain, toh unhone pyaar se inkar kiya (pushed back): "Yeh sirf problems ke solutions dhoondhna nahi hai. Yeh duniya mein kya ho sakta hai (what might possibly exist) use samne lana hai. Aap iske sath jodne (attach) ke liye koi problem dhoondh sakte hain, lekin yeh bas ek achi baat (nice to have) hai." Artists nayi possibilities generate karte hain jinme potential (dam) hota hai. Unhe abhi tak nahi pata ki yeh dilchasp (interesting) kyun lag raha hai, lekin wo dekh sakte hain ki yeh dilchasp hai. Jab aap yeh dekhna seekh jate hain ki kya dilchasp hai, toh aap jante hain ki jo aapne notice kiya hai use follow karna hai aur us raste par chalna shuru karna hai. Agar raste mein kuch faydemand (useful) nikal kar aata hai, toh kaam khatam hone ke baad, aap shayad us problem ko pehchan (identify) sakein jise aapne solve kiya hai. Lekin kaam us problem ko solve karne ke goal ke sath generate nahi kiya gaya tha.

CalArts ke Jeff Keedy is baat se sehmat the ki term "problem solving" bahut linear (seedha) hai, outcome (nateeje) par bahut zyada focused hai. Wo "opportunities (mauko)" ke baare mein baat karna pasand karte hain, aur wo umeed karte hain ki designer "asal mein zaruri taur par problem ko ek naya roop (redefine) de sake." Anther Kiley, jo CalArts mein hi hain, unhone mujhe bataya ki mid-twentieth century ke modernist graphic designers khud ko "visual problem solvers" mante the. "Tab se field aur charcha (discourse) mein badlav (evolution) aaya hai; ab, designers problem solvers hone ke sath-sath provocateurs (uksane wale) aur problem makers (banane wale) bhi hain."

Artists aur designers jis tarah problems solve karte hain wo us tareeqe se alag hai jaise hum mein se zyada-tar log apna kaam pura karte hain. Art aur design professors chahte hain ki students un problems ko solve kar sakein jo abhi tak exist (maujood) nahi kartin ya aisi problems jo saaf taur par samajh nahi aayin. Professors *ill-structured problems* (bina soche-samjhe) ya *open-ended problems* dekar problem finding (problem dhoondhna) sikhate hain. Ek well-designed assignment ke sath, students ko problem ki apni khud ki pehchaan (formulation) banani hoti hai aur phir isey solve karna hota hai. Art aur design projects open-ended hote hain taaki har student ko assignment ke dauran kaam karte hue apni khud ki problem formulate karni pade. Hum iske baare mein chapters 6 aur 7, "Constraints (Rukawatein)" aur "Ambiguity (Uljhan)" mein aur zyada seekhenge. Creativity researchers isey *problem finding* kehte hain, aur yeh behtar creativity se juda (associated) hai.

Amy Thompson, Washington University in St. Louis (WashU) mein ek book designer, assignments ko is tarah design karti hain ki students ko assignment par kaam karte hue apni khud ki problem dhoondhni pade. Hum book design ki ek second-year class ke baare mein baat kar rahe the jahan main abhi gaya tha. "Main ek project doongi, aur phir us project ke andar un sabke alag-alag sawal aur problems honge. Main unhe ek individual (akela) level par hal (address) karti hoon jaise-jaise wo samne aate hain. Class ke aakhir mein jo aapne observe kiya, aap waqt ke sath-sath development (vikas) dekh sakte the, course ki disha (trajectory); aakhir mein, aap sach mein dekh sakte the ki wo us jagah tak pahunchne ke liye jahan wo aakhir mein pahunche, unhone kitni saari problems ke zariye kaam kiya. Mujhe wo class padhana waqayi pasand tha. Har project apne se pehle wale project par banta (built) tha."

Maine unse poocha, "Jab aap kehti hain ki students problems solve kar rahe hain, toh kya aapka matlab un problems se hai jo aapne di thin ya un problems se jo unke apne process se ubhar kar (emerged) aayin?" Unhone jawab diya, "Mujhe lagta hai dono, wo problems jo hum unhe dete hain aur sath hi wo problems jo project par kaam karne ke process se guzarte hue samne aati hain." Jab humne is baare mein baat ki toh Thompson bahut excited ho gayin, aur phir unhone samjhaya kyun: unki khud ki college thesis problem solving aur art aur science ke rishte ke baare mein thi. Unhone biology aur art mein double major kiya tha:

*Meri thesis problem solving aur khubsurati (beauty) ke baare mein thi. Problem aur problem ko solve karne mein khubsurati kaise hoti hai, aur problem ke solution mein, lekin solution ki talaash (pursuit) mein bhi. Mujhe lagta hai mere art students yahi kar rahe hain. Wo un problems ke alawa jo hum unhe dete hain, apne liye bhi problems bana (creating) rahe hain, aur phir jis process se wo unhe solve karte hain wahi learning process hai.*

### **Idea ka Intezar Na Karein; Bas Shuru Karein (Don't Wait for an Idea; Just Get Started)**

**Materials bhi utne hi engaged (shamil) aur activated hain jitni main hoon. Mera is baat par kam control hota hai ki outcome (nateeja) kya hoga, ya aakhiri point (endpoint) kya hoga yeh tay karne par bhi. Endpoint aam taur par ek material function se bahar aata hai, jahan material bas kehta hai "bas (enough)," aap jante hain. Mere liye isme se bahut kuch khoj (discovery) hai, kyunki mujhe nahi pata hota ki kya hone wala hai.**

Lily Kuonen, MFA painting student, SCAD

Hum Lily Kuonen ke studio mein baithe the, jahan unke kaam deewaron par faile (covering) hue the (dekhein figure 3.3). Yeh SCAD ke MFA program mein unke dusre saal ka March tha, aur unka thesis ka kaam bas kuch hi mahinon mein jama karna (due) tha. Wo apne creative safar (journey) ko bayan kar rahi thin. Jab unhone dedh saal pehle program shuru kiya tha,

toh wo portraits aur figures (shaklein) paint kar rahi thin, jaisa ki figure 3.3 mein sabse upar dikhaya gaya hai. Lekin unhone dheere-dheere badalna shuru kiya: "Main jo bana rahi thi usse main khush nahi thi, lekin mujhe pakka nahi pata tha ki main kya karna chahti thi. Main dhoondh rahi thi ki main aage kya karne wali hoon. Main lagatar is baat ko analyze karne ki koshish kar rahi thi ki main kis baat se na-khush thi." Unhone experiment karna shuru kiya, jiska idea (prompted) unhe Formal Aspects of Painting class mein ek assignment se mila: do din mein pachaas (50) artworks banayein. Unmein se kuch experiments figure 3.3 mein deewar par lage hue hain. Unhone angoor ke danthal (grape stems) ko pantyhose (ek tarah ki socks) mein bhar diya. Unhone ek teabag liya us cup se jise unhone abhi piya tha, use pink paint mein duboya, aur us par chalk ki dhool (chalk dust) chhidak di. Unhone furniture aur ice cream cones ki paintings banayin. Achanak, unhe ehsaas hua ki wo portraits banane se na-khush kyun thin: "Oh! Main samajh gayi (I got it)! Wahi wo pal (moment) tha jahan mujhe ehsaas hua ki main painting mein aakhiri image par bahut zyada nirbhar (relying) thi, chahe wo figurate (kisi ki shakal) ho ya kuch aur. Mujhe ehsaas hua ki mujhe is activity mein shamil hone (engagement) mein dilchaspi thi, aakhiri nateeje (finality) mein nahi." Unhone apne is naye ehsaas (emergent realization) ke liye ek naam rakha: PLAYNTINGS, "play (khelna)" aur "painting" ka ek combination. Yeh shabd is baat par zor deta hai ki painting ek active aur ubharne wala (emergent) process hai, jo kabhi khatam nahi hota, kabhi final nahi hota.

**FIGURE 3.3** Lily Kuonen ka studio, Savannah College of Art and Design. 2010.

Maine SCAD mein graphic design ki professor, Rhonda Arntsen ka interview unke campus office mein liya. Maine poocha, aap students ko aakhiri point (endpoint) par focus karna band karne aur unke kaam ko process se ubharne (emerge) ka mauka dene mein kaise madad kar sakti hain? "Yahan tak ki agar mere paas koi aisa student aata hai jo sach mein kuch shandaar (brilliant) lekar aata hai, aur aap nishabd (speechless) ho jate hain, jaise 'Oh my gosh! Yeh kamal (remarkable) hai!' Aur us pal bhi, aapko rukna hoga aur peeche hatna hoga aur student ko un paraton (layers) ko hatane mein madad karni hogi taaki poochne ke liye ek aur sawal mil sake, taaki wo isme aur gehrai (deeper) tak jayen." Unhone apne desk se ek recent student project ka ek example dikhane ke liye hath aage badhaya (reached over), ek wedding invitation (shadi ka card) jo bahut khubsurati se banaya gaya tha. Khubsurat, lekin phir bhi . . .

*Yeh ek design hai jo mere ek student ne apni shadi ke liye banaya tha. Mere paas ek magnet hai, mere paas ek watercolor painting hai, mere paas digital typography (likhayi) hai—aap jante hain, kisi aisi cheez ke bajaye jise aap bas kholte hain, ek object ka banana (construction). Usne yahan ek experience banaya hai. Yeh date aur in sabhi cheezon ko dikhane (reveal) ke liye slide karke khulta hai. Aur phir humne poocha, "Khair, experience ka kya?" Main kahungi, "Khair, is phool ka kya matlab hai? Yeh kis tarah ka phool hai? Iska historical representation (tarikhi matlab) kya hai?" Bhale hi yeh behtareen aur khubsurat hai, main unse aur poochungi, shayad is baare mein ki image ka kya matlab hai. Ya iski banawat (construction) ke baare mein. Yeh interactive (jiske sath juda ja sake) kyun hai? Yeh ek phool kyun hai? Yeh interact kyun karta hai, aur yeh lagbhag hinsa (violence) kyun karta hai? Bhale hi yeh mazedar (fun) hai aur mujhe sach mein ek badiya experience deta hai, yeh phool ki image ko kharab (disrupts) kar deta hai. Toh, ise is tarah ek sath kyun rakha gaya hai? Yeh ek lakdi (stick) ke bajaye magnet kyun hai? Toh yeh zaroor hona chahiye ki, main isey refrigerator par laga dunga. Lekin phir, yeh ek phool kyun hai? Main lagatar, lagatar, aur lagatar sabse choti detail ke baare mein pooch rahi hoon.*

Students ke liye unka learning goal yeh hai ki wo inmein se har decision ko samjha (explain) sakein. Unhe conscious (hosh) taur par pata hona chahiye ki unhone kya kiya hai; yeh sirf artistic intuition (andar ki aawaz) ya na samjhaye ja sakne wali feeling ki baat nahi hai. Aapko ache ideas chahiye, lekin aapko unhe samjhana bhi padega.

*Intuition (Andar ki awaz) ka isme bahut bada role hota hai, lekin creative log, chahe wo scientists hon ya musicians ya visual artists ya physicians, isse koi farq nahi padta, jo cheez sachhe (true) creative logon ko alag (separates) karti hai wo yeh janna hai ki intuition maujood hai, lekin phir intuition ko samajhne aur intuition ko lafzon mein bayan (articulate) karne ke kabil hona taaki aap lagatar ek discovery (khoj) ke mode mein rahein jo phir creativity ko aage badhata (feeds) hai.*

WashU mein ek MFA student, Thurston Belmer, is improvisational (mauqe par kuch banana) irade (intention) ke virodhabhas (paradox) ko samjhate hain:

*Agar aakhir mein painting ka matlab wahi nikalta hai jo aapne shuru mein chaha tha, toh mere hisab se isme kuch galat hai. Iska matlab hai ki aapne kuch nahi seekha. Aapne bilkul bhi aage nahi badhe (grow). Painting aapke sath grow honi chahiye. Kaam karte waqt aapko khud ko educate (sikhana) karna chahiye.*

### **The Unfolding Process (Khulta Hua Process)**

Main Laura Victore se unke studio mein March mein mila, jo SCAD mein MFA student ke taur par unke dusre aur aakhiri saal ka spring (basant) tha. Wo pichle kaam (past work) ke chote-chote tukdon, MFA program mein apne waqt ke kai experiments se ghiri hui thin. Hum us creative process ke baare mein baat kar rahe the jo unke aakhiri MFA thesis project ki taraf le gaya. Unhone asmaan mein monoliths (bade pattharon) ki charcoal representations (tasweerein) banakar shuruat ki thi. Unka idea ek bhari (heavy) object ke tairne (flotation) ko darshana (represent) tha. "Main bas kaam mein ek feeling lane ki koshish kar rahi thi; maine sirf visual (dekhne wali) cheezon se shuruat ki thi." Unhone alag-alag tarah ke materials aur tools ke sath experiment kiya. Unhone Photoshop mein tairte hue monoliths ki kuch images banayin. "Mujhe pakka nahi pata tha ki yeh sab kahan ja raha hai. Yeh sach hai. Yeh sach hai. Isme se bahut kuch tab develop hua jab cheezein aage badhin." Bhari cheez ke tairne ke idea par kaam karte waqt, unhe "beautiful demise (khubsurat tabahi)": dur mustaqbil (distant future) mein ek aese waqt ko darshane (representing) ka idea aaya jab zameen (earth) insaani zindagi ko bachaye rakhne (sustain) ke qabil nahi rahegi. Isse yeh idea paida hua ki monoliths insaniyat ke khatam hone (extinction) ke monuments (yaadgarein) ho sakte hain. Unhone mujhe bataya, "Main darasal isey ek khubsurat (beautiful) cheez ke taur par celebrate kar rahi hoon. Yeh thoda ajeeb (sick) hai, mujhe lagta hai. [Hasti hain.]" Maine poocha, kya idea pehle aaya, ya image pehle aayi? "Sare chote-chote hisse (bits and pieces) ek sath thoda-thoda kaam kar rahe the, ek tarah se khud ko ek sath bun (knitting) rahe the."

Victore ne mujhe apne process ke shuruati daur ke ek studio critique ke baare mein bataya. Wo class mein monolith ki ek badi painting le aayi thin. Professor aur students ne is baare mein baat ki ki kaise is vishal (huge) monolith ko kisi sach mein choti cheez ke taur par dobara tasavvur (reconceived) kiya ja sakta hai. Unhone ishara kiya ki iski shakal (shape) ek Wii ya Gameboy ya un purani external disk drives mein se ek jaisi thi. Disk drive ke sujhaav (suggestion) ne ek naya idea paida (triggered) kiya: "yeh kisi dusre wajood (being) ko communication (baat-cheet) karne ka ek zariya (vehicle) ho sakta hai." Unhone bade monoliths ko bahut chote objects ke taur par dobara concept (reconceptualized) diya. Lekin ek baar jab wo chote ho gaye, toh size ne potential meanings (mumkin matalib) ka ek alag hi set pesh kiya. Ab wo monuments (yaadgarein) nahi ho sakte the. Is naye tasavvur (conception) mein, khubsurat tabahi (beautiful demise) ke baad, jab saare insaan chale gaye, toh yeh chote "monoliths" aese objects the jinhe insaano ne is baat ke saboot (evidence) ke taur par chhoda tha ki wo maujood the, is baat ka saboot ki wo kaun the.

Unhone mujhe chote information storage devices ke Photoshop images, sketches, aur 3D printed objects dikhaye. Yeh ek bahut bada kaam tha; main impress tha. Unke MFA thesis show ke liye display karne ke liye yeh zaroor kafi hota. Lekin phir wo ek aur shelf ki taraf mudi, jo usi imagery (tasweero) par based glass sculptures se bhari hui thi. Aisa lag raha tha ki yeh ek poori tarah se alag MFA show bhi ho sakta hai. Lekin wo wahan bhi nahi ruki thin; unhone mujhe aur objects—paper par images, metal sculptures, linen par oil paintings dikhane ke liye ek aur shelf ki taraf mud kar dekha. Painting aam taur par canvas par ki jati hai, isliye maine unse poocha ki wo linen par painting kyun kar rahi hain. Iska koi pehle se tay irada (prior intention) nahi tha; yeh "bas trial aur error (koshish aur galti)" tha. Unhone do alag-alag materials par ek hi jaise painting banayi: linen par ek choti, canvas par ek badi. "Linen hath ke sath itna reactive (asar-daar) tha, isne rang ko behtar dikhaya. Main samjha nahi sakti. Jaise lakhon cheezein . . ." unki aawaz madham (trailed off) pad gayi. Unki chhattis-page (36-page) ki MFA thesis ka title *The beautiful demise* tha aur isme postmodernist Jean-Franòois Lyotard ki kitab *The inhuman* ka analysis kiya gaya tha, sath hi ise mazhabi kahanion (religious narratives), space travel, reliques (nishaniyon) ki taqat, aur modernist International Style se joda gaya tha. Lekin inmein se koi bhi concept kaam se pehle nahi aaya tha; inmein se kisi bhi theory ne process ko nahi chalaya (drove) tha. Kaam process se ubhar kar (emerged) aaya, aur phir meaning (matlab) ko peechay ja kar (retroactively) joda gaya. Yahi *irade ka weham (the illusion of intentionality)* hai: kaam poora hone ke baad, aisa lagta hai jaise iske piche koi irada (intention) tha. Lekin kaam hone se pehle, yeh irada nahi tha.

### **Retrospective Interpretation (Baad mein Matlab Nikalna)**

**Ubharne wali cheez (The emergent), jab wo zahir hoti hai, toh hamesha pichle waqt (past) ka nateeja (follow from) payi jati hai, lekin uske zahir hone se pehle, definition (tareef) ke lihaz se, yeh past ka nateeja nahi hoti.**

Pragmatist philosopher G. H. Mead, *The philosophy of the present*, 1932

**Kabhi-kabhi yeh cheezein bas achanak aa (appear) jati hain. Yeh bas un cheezon mein se ek hai jo ho gayi.**

Jennifer Baker, MFA student at Washington University in St. Louis

Art mein meaning (matlab) pakadna mushkil (elusive) hai, yahan tak ki artist ke liye bhi. Art critics aur gallery curators artworks ko meaning (matlab) dekar guzara karte hain. Unki writings ki saaf-goi (seeming clarity) is sachai ko chupati (belies) hai: meaning kaam se pehle nahi aata; meaning kaam karne ke process se ubhar kar (emerges) aata hai. Kaam poora hone ke baad hi us par baad mein (retrospectively) meaning thopa (imposed) jata hai.

**Main ek irade (intention) ke sath shuruat karta hoon ki mujhe kya chahiye, lekin banane (making) ke dauran, mujhe is baat ke liye khula (open) rehna padta hai ki kaam kahan ja raha hai. Main apni marzi (will) thop sakta hoon aur painting se wahi karwa sakta hoon jo main chahta hoon, ya main isey aage badhne (evolving) de sakta hoon aur yeh nahi janne ki azaadi de sakta hoon ki yeh kahan ja raha hai. Yeh sabse zyada exciting (dilchasp) cheez hai kyunki yeh wo nahi hota jo maine socha tha ki main banaunga. Phir aap baithte hain aur kaam ko dekhte hain, aur aap yeh samajhne (figure out) ki koshish karte hain ki kya hua. Concepts kaam ko dekhne aur uske sath jeene se aate hain.**

Steve DeFrank, first-year foundations, SVA

Victore ki likhi hui thesis hamein batati hai ki unke kaam ka matlab kya hai: "Maine aesa ek landscape banaya hai jahan dur mustaqbil (distant future) mein is zameen aur in imaginary information units (khayali jankari ki ekayion) se milna kaisa hoga . . . agar aap is grah (planet) par wapas aane wale ek survivor (zinda bachne wale) hon." Lekin—sabhi artists ki tarah—dono objects *aur unka meaning* materials ke sath kaam karne ke ek iterative process se ubhar kar aaye. Lyotard ki kitab *The inhuman* ke sath unka conceptual (khayali) link kaam hone ke baad hi mumkin ho paya. Ek insaan jo yeh nahi janta ki art kaise kaam karta hai, wo yeh soch kar gumrah (misled) ho sakta hai ki monolith ka idea, chaliye man lete hain (let's say), unke religious narratives (mazhabi kahaniyon) ke analysis se aaya, lekin yeh bahut hi sadgi bhari soch (simplistic) hai. Artists aur designers ke liye, ideas process se ubharte hain, aur process khatam hone ke baad, meaning baad mein (retrospectively) lagaya jata hai.

Yeh hamesha artist nahi hota jo baad mein (retroactively) meaning lagata hai; yeh aksar art critic ya gallery dealer hota hai. Jab aap kisi art museum mein jate hain ya jab aap kisi artist ki website padhte hain, toh aap is baare mein padhenge ki artist ke kaam ka kya matlab hai. Shayad artist gender, ya dukh (sadness), ya nuksan (loss), ya motherhood (maatritva) ko explore kar raha ho. Kabhi-kabhi aap padhenge ki kaam mein ek political message chhipa hai—nasl (race), zulm (oppression), gareebi (poverty), maholyati badlav (environmental change) ke baare mein. Ek aam belief (vishwas) hai ki ek artwork artist ki taraf se ek message communicate (bhejne) karne ki koshish hoti hai; ki artist ka goal (maqsad) kaam ke liye us irade (intent) ko darshana (represent) hai. Haan, kai artists is tarah kaam karte hain. Lekin pehle se soche gaye (premeditated) irade ko nakarna (rejection) aaj ke (contemporary) art world mein leaders ke statements se mel khata (consistent) hai. Misaal ke taur par, mashhoor art critic Dave Hickey (1938–2021) ne kaha, "Mujhe kisi artist ke iradon (intentions) ki parwah nahi hai. Mujhe is baat ki parwah hai ki kaam aisa lagta hai ki iske kuch nateeje (consequences) ho sakte hain."

Kara Rooney ne School of Visual Arts (SVA) mein pandrah (15) saal tak padhaya hai, aur haal hi mein Rhode Island School of Design (RISD) mein bhi padhaya hai. Wo ek multidisciplinary artist hain jo installation performance, video photography, painting, aur sculpture ke sath kaam karti hain. Wo kabhi-kabhi dusre artists aur musicians ke sath milkar (collaboration) kaam karti hain. Wo ek published art critic bhi hain, aur wo kai exhibitions ki guest curator bhi rahi hain. Unhone women's studies aur feminist theory mein minor kiya hai. Wo is idea ke bilkul khilaf (opposed) nahi hain ki artworks ka meaning (matlab) hota hai. Lekin Rooney bhi students ko yeh dekhna sikhati hain ki meaning process se kaise ubhar kar aata (emerges) hai. Dimaag mein ek irada hona kafi nahi hai; wo irada kaam mein dikhna (happening) chahiye. Unhone mujhe ek student ke baare mein bataya jisne kaha, "Main environment ke baare mein kaam karna chahta hoon." Rooney ne jawab diya:

*"Yeh badiya (wonderful) hai, yeh theek hai, lekin yeh tumse kaise judta (connect) hai? Mujhe kuch aesa nahi chahiye jo ek aur fifth-grade ke science experiment jaisa dikhe. Mujhe kuch aesa chahiye jo sach mein mujhe tumhare baare mein artist ke taur par kuch bataye." Hum unke final ke liye environment ke us idea par wapas aa sakte hain, lekin unhe us par wapas aane se pehle kuch steps se guzarna pada hai, jahan unhone sach mein un layers (parton) aur mask (mukhoto) ko hata (peeled away) diya hai. Wo samajhte hain ki public audience ke samne zyada vulnerable (kamzor/imandar) hona kya hota hai, kaam ke baare mein mushkil sawalon ka jawab dena aur aalochna (criticism) ko lena kya hota hai.*

WashU mein MFA student Thurston Belmer ne us process ko describe kiya jo unki thesis ke ubharne (emergence) ki wajah bana. Yeh intellectual (samajhdari ka) ya theoretical (kitabi) ya raaz-bhara (mysterious) nahi tha. Yeh ek iterative process mein materials ke sath kaam karne ki rozana ki practice (daily practice) thi: "Maine aahista-aahista us cheez par react karna shuru kiya jo main dekh raha tha. Bas bahut hi baarik (minutely) dhang se, yeh aage badha (progressed). Badkismati se (unfortunately) yeh kuch zyada dramai (dramatic) nahi hai." Maine poocha, art school mein undergraduate ke taur par chaar saal aur MFA student ke taur par ek saal ke baad, aapke kaam mein ab kya alag (different) hai? Farq yeh hai ki usne dekhna seekh liya hai.

*Main maslon (issues) ko zyada saaf (clearly) pehchanta hoon. Yahan mera experience har us cheez ko hatana (removing) raha hai jise ek asaan samajh (easy read) ya symbol (nishani), ya ghisa-pita (trite) samjha ja sake. Kyunki jaise hi aapke paas yeh cheezein hoti hain, koi bhi saaf (clear) message ya symbol, painting ka experience wahin ruk jata hai aur yeh kuch aesa ban jata hai jise aap novel ki tarah padhna chahenge. Yeh kuch aesa ban jata hai jise samjha (figured out) ja sake. Main pasand karta hoon (prefer) ki shuruat se hi yeh socha jaye, "yeh samajh se bahar (incomprehensible) hai." Mere paas iska ek idea ho sakta hai ki yeh kya hai, lekin main yeh nahi keh sakta, "Yeh wahi hai," agar is baat ka koi matlab nikalta ho (if that makes sense). Jaise yeh mere liye kya mayne rakhta hai, ya mujhe kya lagta hai iska kya matlab ho sakta hai, lekin kabhi bhi yeh kehne ki sambhavna (possibility) nahi hoti ki iska yahi matlab hai, ya artist yahi keh raha hai. Meri images kisi aisi cheez se door ho gayi hain jise padha ja sake (readable). Main read ko jitna slow down kar saku, utna behtar hai.*

"The read" se, Belmer ka matlab yeh hai ki dekhne wala (viewer) kaam ke sath kaise judta (engages) hai. Wo nahi chahte ki kaam ka koi ek khas meaning (matlab) kaam mein saaf taur par (obviously) dikhai de. Ek kamyaab (successful) art ke kaam mein, dekhne wale ke liye meaning uske kaam ke samne (encounter) aane se ubharta (emerges) hai. Isne mujhe pragmatist (haqeeqat pasand) philosopher John Dewey ki yaad dila di, jinhone *Art as experience* (1934) mein art ko deewar par latke ek static object ke taur par nahi, balki dekhne wale ke ek tajurbe (experience) ke taur par analyze kiya tha. Dewey ki theories Philadelphia mein Barnes Foundation ki curation (dekhbhal) ke piche ek important driving force (taqat) thin, jo ek museum hai jo apne impressionist aur modern paintings ke liye jana jata hai. Dewey ka man-na tha ki ek painting ko dekhte waqt, dekhne wale ko wahi experience hota hai jo artist ko kaam create karte waqt hua tha. Kaam aur meaning dono ek sath (in parallel), ek improvisational process se, an-soche dhang se (unpredictably) ubharte hain. Jab kaam ko display kiya jata hai aur ek audience ise dekhti hai, toh kaam ka meaning dekhne wale ke liye uske kaam ke samne (encounter) aane par ubharta hai. Agar dekhne wala kaam ko jaldi samajh (understand) sakta hai, toh yeh ek problem hai, Dewey ne dawa kiya, kyunki iska matlab hai ki kaam un saajhi (shared) clichés (ghisi piti baton) par based hai jinse dekhne wala pehle se hi waqif (familiar) hai. Iska yeh bhi matlab hai ki artist ne shayad ubharne ke ek iterative process ko follow karne ke bajaye, kaam ke meaning ka faisla kafi jaldi kar liya tha.

1934 mein, Dewey ne yeh shabd likhe the, jo un professors aur students ke ideas ki tarah hi lagte hain jinka maine interview liya tha:

*Art ka product (nateeja) art ka kaam (work of art) nahi hai. Ek aakhiri product ka sakht pehle se tay hona (rigid pre-determination) ek mechanical (machinery) ya academic product ki taraf le jata hai. An-socha (unexpected) mod (turn), kuch aesa jiska artist khud saaf taur par andaza nahi lagata (definitely foresee), wahi art ke kaam ki khubsurat quality ki ek shart (condition) hai; yeh isey mechanical hone se bachata hai.*

1934 mein John Dewey ki tarah, Candida Alvarez (SAIC) kehti hain ki material object creativity ka outcome (nateeja) nahi hai; process apne aap mein wahi hai jo artists create kar rahe hain:

*Process hi kaam (work) hai. Yeh kaam tak pahunchta hai. Kaam sirf aakhiri product (end product) nahi hai.*

1938 mein, aesthetic philosopher R. G. Collingwood ne bhi object ke upar process ki ehmiyat par zor (emphasized) diya:

*Paint ki gayi tasveer art ka kaam nahi hai. Halanki, iska banna kisi na kisi tarah zaroori roop se (necessarily) aesthetic activity (khubsurati wali sargarmi) se juda hua hai, yani, us khayali tajurbe (imaginative experience) ke ban-ne (creation) se jo ki art ka kaam hai. Art ka kaam artist dwara banai gayi koi cheez hai, lekin yeh kisi pehle se soche gaye (preconceived) plan ko pura karke nahi banaya gaya hai, aur na hi kisi pehle se soche gaye maqsad (end) tak pahunchne ke zariye ko haqeekat banakar.*

Emergence (Ubhar kar aana) irade (intention) aur nayi sambhavnaon ke khule-pan (openness) ke beech balance banati hai. Yeh improvisational action hai, jiska andaza nahi lagaya ja sakta (unpredictable), jo waqt ke sath-sath (through time) aur material practice mein shamil (engaged) hota hai. Dekhna seekhna sirf art aur creativity ke baare mein nahi hai. Yeh intention, action, aur meaning ke baare mein hai; yeh is baare mein hai ki ek insaan hone ka kya matlab hai.

### **Bhatakna (Floundering / Struggle Karna)**

**Yeh is baare mein nahi hai ki aap kya sochte hain ki aap kya kar rahe hain. Kaam hamesha ek saboot (evidence) hota hai. Baaki sab khwahish (wishing), ummeed (aspiration), aur irada (intentionality) hai.**

Ron Leax, sculptor, Washington University in St. Louis

Adam Reinhard, SCAD ke ek MFA student, ne mujhe art school mein apne undergraduate saalo ke baare mein bataya: "Jab maine school shuru kiya, toh main samaj (society) ki problems ke baare mein baat karna chahta tha. Main sach mein sochta tha ki main duniya ko badal dunga, ki isse logon ki aankhein khulengi. Maine protest marches—Viet Nam, civil rights—se jude kaam kiye. Main sach mein pair (foot) ke nishan (motif) ki taraf khincha chala jata tha. High school mein meri paintings mein pair hamesha hote the." Pair, unke liye, protest marches ki yaad dilate the. "Main waqayi, waqayi mein passionate (junooni) tha. Maine uske baare mein apni grad school application mein likha tha. Social change hamesha us art ki reedh ki haddi (backbone) raha hai jise main create karne ki koshish karta raha hoon. Maine social change wale pehlu (aspect) ke liye apna junoon (passion) banaye rakha hai, lekin mujhe lagta hai ki main is haqeeqat (fact) ke baare mein zyada samajhdar (enlightened) ho gaya hoon ki log un cheezon ko nazar-andaz (disregard) karne lagte hain jo bahut asani se mil jati hain." MFA program mein dakhil hone ke kuch hi waqt baad, unhone ek personal transformation (niji badlaav) ka tajurba kiya; nayi aur an-sochi (unexpected) possibilities (sambhavnayein) ubharna (emerge) shuru huin:

*Mere pehle do quarters mein, main apni sabse badi artistic crisis se guzra. Meri koi disha (direction) nahi thi. Main bas images (tasweerein) pakad raha tha. Main college ke baad apne ek photographer dost (jiske sath main room share karta tha) ke banaye gaye images ko palat (flipping through) raha tha. Uske paas ek fisheye lens se li gayi yeh self-portrait (apni tasveer) thi. Aur maine socha "Wow, yeh cool hai!" Maine kabhi fisheye lenses ke baare mein nahi socha tha. Yeh poori tarah se ek accident (ittefaq) tha. Yeh tab hua jab main ideas ke liye bhatak (floundering) raha tha. Main literally apni sketchbook lekar baithta aur, bas do ghante ke liye, aese ideas draw karta jo shayad kaam kar jayen, jinhe main samajh saku (percolate) aur unke baare mein soch saku. Toh maine fisheye image ka print nikala aur lagbhag pentalis (45) minute mein ise paint kar liya. Maine ise dekha, aur kaha, "Isme kuch taqat (power) hai."*

Reinhard ek painter the aur fisheye lens ka samna unhe isliye hua kyunki wo ek photographer roommate ke sath reh rahe the jisne iska istemal kiya tha. Unhone fisheye painting ke potential ko explore karna shuru kiya, yeh jane bina ki iska kya matlab (meaning) hai, lekin yeh ehsaas (sense) rakhte hue ki yeh dilchasp (interesting) ho sakta hai.

Ek aur experiment ke taur par, unhone adult situations mein bacchon ki paintings banayin. Fisheye painting ki tarah, wo koi message communicate karne ki koshish nahi kar rahe the; yeh bas dilchasp lag raha tha. Yahan dekhiye kyun:

*Meri ek choti behen thi jo college mein meri padhai ke dauran aath (8) se barah (12) saal ki thi. Maine socha ki ek bacche ko kisi adult (bade) situation mein rakh kar main logon ko dikha sakta hoon ki yeh kitna ajeeb (absurd) hai. Misaal ke taur par, main do saal ki ek bachi ko workaholic (kaam ke piche pagal) maa ke kapdon mein dikhata, jisne apni jewelry aur pantsuit aur heels pehni hon aur Working Mother magazine padh rahi ho.*

Maine unse poocha, "Aapko yeh idea kaise mila?"

*Yeh ek tarah se bhatakne (floundered) jaisa hai, jab tak ki kisi cheez ne is idea ko josh (spark) nahi diya. Mujhe nahi pata wo kya tha. Mujhe pata tha ki main kisi cheez ke kareeb hoon (on to something), lekin maine ise thodi der theharne (let it rest) diya. Maine kuch aur cheezein paint ki. Meri kuch aur classes thin. Is dauran, main in sky space (asmaani jagah) mein girte hue bacchon ki paintings kar raha tha, jo bas space mein ghoom (tumbling) rahe the. Yeh funny tha kyunki yeh ek tarah se khud par hi ishara (self-referential) kar raha tha. Mera matlab hai, main apne kaam mein bhatak (floundering) raha tha. Yeh aesa tha ki, hum kahan land karne wale hain (girne wale hain)?*

Sabhi MFA painting students apne studios mein kaam karte hain jo ek dusre ke kareeb hote hain. Wo process ke dauran (in progress) ek dusre ka kaam dekhte hain, har exploration aur har galti ko. Wo ek dusre ko support karte hain, lekin wo sachhe (honest) bhi hote hain. Unhone Reinhard ko bataya ki wo girte huye bacchon se inspire nahi huye. Reinhard ko qubool (admit) karna pada ki wo bhi nahi the. "Main isey lekar josh mein (psyched) nahi tha. Yeh bas khali jagah bharne (filler) ke liye tha. Main bas isey aazma (trying) raha tha, aur yeh kaam nahi kar raha tha."

Unhone photographs ko paint karne ke idea ke sath aur experiment karne ka faisla kiya. Kai experiments ke baad, kaam mein meaning (matlab) samne aane (emerge) laga (dekhein figure 3.4).

**FIGURE 3.4**

Adam Reinhard. *Pass the Buck*. Canvas par Oil. 2009.

*Maine upar se, logon par niche ki taraf se photos lena shuru kiya, aur sir (head) bada tha, yeh iski hadd (extremity) hai. Phir, maine poocha, "Yeh kya hasil (achieving) kar raha hai?" Aur mujhe ehsaas hua, "Yahan hai mera social change!" Mujhe ehsaas hua ki agar main kisi ko fisheye lens se niche ki taraf dekh raha hoon, toh main us par hawi (towering over) ho raha hoon aur us par kisi cheez ka ilzaam (accusing) laga raha hoon. Maine ilzaam (blame) aur jawabdehi (accountability) ke concepts ke baare mein aur yeh sochne laga ki yeh hamari saari problems ki jad (root) kaise hai. Log ilzaam lagate hain, ilzaam apne sar lete hain, apni galti dusron par dalte (passing the buck) hain.*

*Yeh mujhe achanak (all of a sudden) se aagaya, lekin tab jab maine ideas likhne, kisi image ke conveys (diye gaye) messages par soch-vichar karne mein bahut waqt bitaya tha. Main soch raha tha, agar yeh image sirf mazedar (fun) hoti, toh main isme se kya nikal (squeeze out) sakta tha? Fisheye wali painting banane ke lagbhag teen hafte baad ki baat hai. Maine isey bas wahin chhod diya aur yeh bas pak rahi thi (percolating). Yeh bas ho raha tha. Maine isey kabhi kisi class mein dikhaya tak nahi. Mujhe bas laga ki yeh cool thi. Toh, maine khud se poocha, main iski taraf kyun khicha chala (drawn) raha hoon? Agar main ise pehchan (identify) saku, toh yeh ek uchhalne ki jagah (springboard) ban jayega. Us painting mein kuch aesa tha jo wo kar rahi thi. Baki kisi aur painting se mujhe waisi feeling nahi mil rahi thi. Yeh meri zubaan par tha. Main lagbhag taste kar sakta hoon ki isme kya successful (kamyaab) hai.*

Wheelock ke an-dekhe jhaadi (unseen bush) ki tarah, Reinhard ki niji (personal) aur artistic daryaft (discovery) ka rasta materials ke sath kaam karne aur jo ho raha tha use dekhne mein chhupa tha. Dhyan dijiye ki wo personal discovery ke liye kin shabdon ka istemal karte hain:

*Isme kuch taqat (power) hai. Us painting mein kuch aesa tha jo wo kar rahi thi. Yeh bas ho raha tha.*

*Main lagbhag taste kar sakta hoon ki isme kya successful hai.*

Yeh wahi language (bhasha) hai jo aap tab istemal karte hain jab aap dekhna jante hain. Aap kaam ke actions (amal) ko apne aap se alag (independent) dekhte hain. Creativity artist ke andar nahi hoti; creativity usme hoti hai jo kaam *kar रहा hai*; jo *ho raha hai*; yeh *kyun* successful hai. Yeh language artist ko dekhne wale (viewer) ke taur par, kaam ke irade (intention) ko paane wale (recipient) ke taur par dekhti hai. Artist ka kirdar (role) process se kya nikal (emerging) raha hai yeh dekhna hai—chabhi (key) dhoondhna.

*Maine us chabhi (key) ko dhoondhne ki koshish ki, aur jab mujhe wo mil gayi, toh jaise baadh ke darwaze (floodgates) khul gaye. Ek baar jab maine us concept ka faisla kar liya hai jo mujhe thodi der ke liye busy rakhega, toh main in chote-chote bursts (dhamako) ko mehsoos karne ke liye azaad (free) hoon. Main iski boundaries (hudood) ko aage kaise badhau (extend)? Main limits ko aage kaise push karu? Main isey aur zyada dilchasp (engaging) kaise banau?*

Ek akeli inspiration kisi career ke liye kafi nahi hoti. Final MFA show ke liye, Reinhard ko sirf ek painting nahi, balki kaamo ka ek poora set (body of work) banana tha.

*Ab mera sawal yeh hai ki, main apni sabse dilchasp bees (20) paintings kaise banau? Bina kisi formula mein fase? Yahi ek wajah hai ki maine falling (girne wali) paintings ko piche chhod diya. Us project mein kafi gehrai (depth) nahi thi. Maine unme se ek ki, aur mujhe ek aur karne ki zaroorat nahi thi. Lekin blame (ilzaam wali) paintings ke sath, itne saare aapsi (interpersonal) behas, tanaav (tensions), chehre ke chote badlaav (facial nuances), body language, gestures hain jo pareshani (bewildered), na-yakeeni (disbelief), depression, gusse (indignation), aur ilzaam (accusation) ko dikha sakte hain. Yeh mujhe lambe waqt tak aage badhate (keep me going) rehne ke liye kafi hai.*

Ek creative career kabhi bhi sirf ek kaam ke baare mein nahi hota; yeh kaamon ke ek poore set (body of work) ke baare mein hota hai. Professional artists hamesha poochte hain: Is kaam mein kitna potential (dam) hai? Kya yeh nayi sambhavnayein (possibilities) kholta hai? Kya yeh mujhe mahinon ya saalo tak poshan (nourish) dega?

Belmer ki tarah, Reinhard ne bhi aasaan meaning (easy read) se bachna seekh liya tha, aur unhe bhi us point tak pahunchne ke liye art school mein kayi saal lage. Unke undergraduate BFA ke chaar saal kafi nahi the: "College mein mere kaam hadd se zyada narrative (kahani batane wale) the. Wo kuch zyada hi saaf (spelling it out) the." Wo jang (war) aur nagrik adhikar (civil rights) ke baare mein kaam the. "Yahan, mujhe lagta hai ki explore karne ke liye an-ginat (infinite) baarikiyan (subtlety) hain." Jab wo baat karte hain toh main samajh sakta hoon ki wo excited hain; main dekh sakta hoon ki unhe apni khud ki excitement ko notice karne ki niji samajh (personal awareness) hai. Art aur design school mein, aap khud ko dekhna utna hi seekhte hain jitna aap kaam ko dekhna seekhte hain. Jaisa ki Arny Nadler ne introduction mein kaha tha, "Wo students cheezon ko ab kabhi pehle ki tarah nahi dekhenge. Hum samajhne ke tareeqe sikha rahe hain jinka istemal wo apne poore aage ke careers mein karenge."

Bahut se students ek adarsh-vadi (idealists) ke taur par art school aate hain. Wo social change ke liye committed (pur-azm) hote hain. Wo farq (difference) laana chahte hain, ek siyasi (political) nazariya communicate (batana) karna chahte hain, ya dekhne wale ko kisi aise issue se aagah (aware) karna chahte hain jiske baare mein unhe lagta hai ki us par kafi dhyan (attention) nahi diya ja raha hai. Lekin kuch students message par itna focus karte hain ki unhe yeh nahi dikhta ki unke kaam mein darasal (actually) kya ho raha hai. Reinhard ko dekhne mein madad karne ke liye studio critique, professor ke guidance ki zaroorat thi: "Mujhe wo aalochna (criticism) tab mili jab humne artist statements ke baare mein baat ki. Maine apne statement ke top par political accountability (siyasi jawabdehi) ke baare mein likha tha, aur unhone kaha, 'khair, hamein lagta hai ki iska zyada talluq interpersonal (logon ke aapas ke) relationships se hai.' Aap bas yeh nahi keh sakte ki aapka kaam kisi bhi cheez ke baare mein hai."

WashU mein MFA printmaking aur book arts ki student, Lauren Cardenas ne mujhe bataya ki unhone yeh program apne kaam ke zariye siyasi (political) aur samajik (social) messages convey (bhejne) karne ke goal ke sath shuru kiya tha. Halanki, jab wo banane (making) ke process mein shamil hui, toh unhe ehsaas hua ki wo yeh nahi kar rahi thin—yeh unke kaam mein nahi ho raha tha. "Main consumerism (cheezein kharidne ki aadat) ke baare mein baat kar rahi thi, lekin jo kaam main kar rahi thi uska talluq ghar (home) ki pehchan (identity) se tha. Pehle semester mein, zahir hai (apparently) yeh aam baat hai. Wo aapse struggle karne (flounder) ki umeed karte hain. Aap yeh pata lagane ki koshish kar rahe hote hain ki aap kiski baat sun-na chahte hain." Ek artist ban-ne ke liye, aap apne kaam ko apni marzi se chalanay par majboor (force) nahi kar sakte; aapko kaam ko sun-na aur jo usse nikal (emerging) raha hai use follow karna hota hai. Students ka apne kaam mein kisi idea ko haqeekat (realize) bananane ki koshish karna aur phir kuch aesa generate karna jo us idea ko communicate na kare, yeh ek aam baat hai. Cardenas ke iterative process mein engage hone ke baad, meaning dheere-dheere samne aya: "Yeh family dynamic (parivar ki halat) aur maa-beti ke rishte (relation) ke baare mein hai. Yeh manic-depressive (dimaghi beemari) beemari aur davaiyon ke taboos (jo cheezein samaj mein mana hain) ke baare mein hai." Unhone socha tha ki wo consumerism ke baare mein kuch keh rahi hongi, lekin ab yeh meaning ka hissa nahi tha. Unhone kaam ki khwahishon (desires) ko sun-na seekh liya tha. Kaam ki apni ek disha (direction) hoti hai, apni ek zindagi hoti hai; meaning us se ubhar (emerges) aata hai.

### **Jo Hua Hai Uske Liye Alfaaz Dhoondhna (Finding Words for What's Happened)**

**Artist statement ko khatam (abolished) kar dena chahiye! Kyunki artists ko khud nahi pata hota ki wo kya kar rahe hain.**

Art critic Dave Hickey

Jab koi artist apna kaam gallery mein bechne ke liye rakhta hai ya jab kaam kisi museum mein display kiya jata hai, toh dekhne wali public umeed karti hai ki wo iske matlab (meaning) ke baare mein padhenge. Jab aap kisi museum mein hote hain, toh aap aksar artwork ke bottom ke kareeb deewar par ek chota sa card dekhte hain—object label. Label aam taur par artist ke naam, kaam ke naam, wo saal jab kaam banaya gaya tha, aur isey banane mein istemal kiye gaye materials se shuru hota hai. Iske baad aksar kuch sentences hote hain jo samjhate (explain) hain ki kaam ka kya matlab hai aur shayad iska tareekhi (historical) aur samajik sandarbh (social context) bhi batate hain. Label lagbhag hamesha exhibition curator (nugran) dwara likha jata hai, na ki artist dwara. Ek bade museum show ya ek hi artist ke kai kaamon ke collection ke liye, museum ek exhibition catalogue bhi publish kar sakta hai—ek aisi jild-band kitab jisme display kiye gaye kaamon ki images ke sath unke meaning par detail commentary hoti hai.

Artists se umeed ki jati hai ki jab wo apne kaam ko apne studio se duniya mein nikalein toh wo iske baare mein ek statement likhein. Kai artists ko yeh karna pasand nahi hota. 2012 ke WashU ke MFA show mein, jab Belmer aur Cardenas ne apna body of work display kiya, toh exhibition catalog mein har artist ke baare mein essays the, lekin yeh essays critics ne likhe the, khud artists ne nahi. Cardenas ke spring thesis show mein black-and-white prints ka ek collection shamil tha—jo un kaamon se bilkul alag (very different) tha jo wo August mein kar rahi thin. Nicholas Tamarkin, jinke paas comparative literature mein PhD aur directing mein MFA hai, unhone Cardenas ke kaam ke baare mein yeh likha:

*Bhale hi yeh maut (mortality) par koi gaur-o-fikar (meditation per se) na ho, lekin yeh kaam hamein kisi aur cheez se baandhta (tethers) hai. Waqt (Time) sirf ek pal ke roop mein maujood hai jab dimaag (mind) ka jism (body) se aakhiri algaav (separation) hone ka point aata hai. Is sense mein yeh kaam shayad kisi aisi cheez ka rehearsal (tayyari) hai jise "be-ant (the infinite)" kaha ja sakta hai.*

Is interpretation (matlab) ko Cardenas ke family dynamic aur manic-depressive illness ke taboos wale statements ke sath milana (reconcile) aasan nahi hai. Lekin meaning (matlab) kaam ke process se ubhar kar (emerges) aata hai. Kaam pehle aata hai aur meaning baad mein. Jab kaam banaya jata hai toh uska koi meaning nahi hota, lekin ek baar jab yeh wajood mein aata hai, toh art ki duniya yeh maang karti hai ki isko koi meaning diya jaye. Wahi baad mein matlab nikalna (retrospective interpretation) hai. Ek baar jab kaam ka wajood hota hai, toh meaning materials aur dekhne walon (viewers) ke dimaagi asar (cultural reception) ke sath tay (negotiated) kiya jata hai.

Hum chapter 2, "Materials" mein MFA student Kathleen Perniciaro se mile the; wo wahi artist hain jo gaanth baandhne (tying knots) ke potential ko explore kar rahi thin, chahe wo ghaas ki patti (blades of grass) se ho ya dhage (string) se. Unka kaam process se nikalne ke baad, unhe abhi bhi is baare mein likhna tha ki unke kaam ka kya matlab (meaning) hai. Wo is baare mein khush nahi thin. Unhe samjhana tha, gaanth kyun (why knots)? Unhe sach mein nahi pata tha. Meaning baad mein samne aaya tha. Ab jabki unka kaam poora ho chuka hai aur display ke liye taiyar hai, wo keh sakti hain ki unka kaam kis baare mein hai: Unhone mujhe bataya yeh "dohraav (repetition), waqt (time), silsilay (sequence)" aur "waqt ke dhanchon (structures of time)... aur us baare mein hai jo hamein un patterns se bahar nikalta hai." Maine unse poocha, aapko yeh idea kaise aya? Kya yeh achanak milne wali koi insight (samajh) thi? Nahi—Belmer, Baker, aur Cardenas ki tarah, kaam shuru karne se pehle unke paas idea nahi tha. Aap shuruat karte hain, kaam jaari rakhte hain, aur phir, "yeh un cheezon ko pehchan-ne (recognizing) ke baare mein hai jo aapke kaam mein baar-baar aati (occurring) hain. Aur phir explain karein ki wo darasal kya hain. Ek purane statement mein, maine kaha tha ki main mousam ki mari huyi (weathered) cheezon mein past ke bache huye hisson (remnants) ke taur par dilchaspi rakhti hoon." Wo packaging, foil, gumball seed pods ke sath kaam kar rahi thin, aur koshish kar rahi thin "unhe kisi nayi cheez se dobara jodne (reconnect) ki taaki wo usse aage ki zindagi ji sakein jiske liye unhe shuru mein banaya gaya tha." In materials ke sath experiment karte waqt, "kuch cheezein thin jo baar-baar samne (coming up) aa rahi thin; jiske baare mein main asal mein baat kar rahi thi wo waqt (time) tha." Lekin baad mein unhe ehsaas hua ki yeh sach mein waqt ke baare mein bhi nahi tha. "Yeh wo cheezein thin jo hamein hamare aam routines se todti (break) hain." Kaam ki apni ek zindagi hoti hai, aur artist statement ki bhi: "Aapka artist statement hamesha badalta rehta hai. Yeh aapke kaam ke sath badalta (fluctuates) hai . . . agar aapke kaam mein badlaav aane lagte hain ya kuch naya samne aata hai, toh aap apne statement ko dobara dekhte (revisit) hain."

Yeh ek behtareen phrase (jumla) hai: *the work starts having changes (kaam mein badlaav aane lagte hain)*. Yeh alfaaz yeh farz karte hain ki yeh artist nahi hai jo in badlaavon ka karan (causing) ban raha hai. Process ki apni ek alag zindagi (own life) hoti hai; bilkul Reinhard ki tarah jo kehte hain ki unke kaam mein kuch "ho raha (happening)" hai aur kaam cheezein "kar raha (doing)" hai. Kaam ki apni khud ki khwahishein aur logic (mantiq) hota hai. Beshak, artist khamosh (passive) nahi hota. Artist kaam ke sath ek partner hota hai, materials ke sath dialogue ke zariye process ko guide karta hai. Lekin artist control mein bhi nahi hota.

Perniciaro chahhengi ki wo koi statement na hi likhein; unhone kaha, "Kash isse koi farq na padta." Lekin: "Isse farq padta hai; aapko art school mein batane (explain) ke kabil hona chahiye ki aap kya kar rahe hain. Agar main apni khud ki practice mein hoti, toh shayad mujhe har waqt khud ko explain karne ki zaroorat nahi padti. Aapke paas ek statement hona chahiye. Jab tak ki aap koi superstar na hon aur koi badi gallery bas aapka kaam dikhana chahti ho. Kabhi-kabhi artists ki taraf se yeh bakwas (bullshit) ho sakti hai aur yeh unhe baad mein samajh aati hai." Zyada aam taur par, koi aur kaam ko interpret (samajhta) karta hai aur batata hai ki uske khayal mein iska kya matlab hai. "Bahut baar, yeh art historians ya critics ki taraf se aata hai, jo iske baare mein likhte hain. Phir artist shayad kahe, 'Oh, haan, ho sakta hai mera yahi matlab raha ho.' Bahut se artists bas apna kaam duniya ke samne rakh dete hain aur duniya iske baare mein baat karti hai, aur yeh wahi ban jata hai." Artist kandhe uchka kar (shrugs) kehta hai, theek hai, agar aap aisa kehte hain.

MFA student Thurston Belmer ne mujhe bataya: "Aapke MFA ki poori body of work kisi na kisi tarah se judi hui (related) honi chahiye. Lekin sach yeh hai ki, kisse banana (spin a yarn) asaan hai. Isme se bahut kuch shabdon ke sath ek khel (game) hai. Aapko har cheez ko ek sath jodne (spin) ka ek chatur (clever) tareeqa mil jata hai. Aap kuch bhi bana (make up) sakte hain." Lekin MFA students kisi ache professor ke samne bakwas (bullshit) karke bach nahi sakte. Accra Shepp, ek photographer jo SVA mein padhate hain, khari baatein karte (blunt) hain:

*Jab graduate students apne artist statements likh rahe hote hain, toh main unse kehta hoon, yeh khwahishon par mabni (aspirational) nahi ho sakta. Aap wo nahi likh sakte jo aap kaam mein chahte hain kyunki log do cheezon mein se ek sochenge—ya toh aap bewaqoof (stupid) hain ya aap jhoote (liar) hain, ya ek bewaqoof jhoote hain. Logon ko kyun nahi pata chalega ki aap jhooth bol rahe hain? Isliye main unse kehta hoon, jo kaam mein hai wahi likho. Yahi wajah hai ki kabhi-kabhi, artist statement likhne se aap kaam ko ek naye tareeqe se samajhne lagte hain jo aapko aage badhne mein madad karta hai.*

Yahi wajah hai ki aapko art school ki zaroorat hoti hai: kyunki professors aapko imandar (honest) rakhenge. Aap jo chahein wo kehkar bach nahi sakte. Aapko ek aesa statement lana hoga jo kaam mein jhalke (reflected). Yeh ek challenge hai kyunki kaam process se nikal kar aaya (emerged) tha. Meaning (matlab) bana (happened); aapne ise nahi banaya. Toh aapko shabd (words) kahan se milte hain? Yahin par aapke professors aate hain. Wo is discipline ko gehrai se (deeply) jante hain. Wo recommend karte hain ki aap un artists ko dekhein jinka kaam aapke jaisa hai. Unhone Laura Victore se kaha ki Italian Renaissance ke Quattrocento daur ki religious paintings ke sath connections dhoondhein, postwar International Style se judi mid-twentieth century ki buildings ko dekhein, aur sculptor Anselm Kiefer ke kaamo ki study karein. Unhone recommend kiya ki wo literary theorist Lyotard ko padhein, kyunki unhone is baare mein likha tha ki "bina jism ke soch (thought) ko kaise mumkin banaya jaye, aesi soch jo insaani jism ki maut ke baad bhi wajood mein (continue to exist) rahti hai" (Victore ki thesis ke page 2 se). Lekin kaam in pichli misaalon (precedents) aur theorists se nikal kar nahi aaya tha; process pehle aaya, materials ek object mein tabdeel hue, aur phir materials ke sath ek dialogue se meaning ubhar kar (emerged) aya.

*Ek artist ke shabdon ko hamesha ehtiyat (cautiously) se liya jana chahiye. Mukammal kaam (finished work) aksar is baat ke liye ajnabi (stranger) hota hai, aur kabhi-kabhi us baat se poori tarah alag (at odds) hota hai jo artist ne shuru mein mehsoos ki thi ya jise wo bayan (express) karna chahta tha. Sabse behter yahi hai ki artist jo kar sakta hai wo karta hai bajaye uske jo wo karna chahta hai. Jung khatam hone (after the battle is over) aur nuksan (damage) ka samna karne ke baad, nateeja hairan-kun hadd tak be-jaan (surprisingly dull) ho sakta hai—lekin kabhi-kabhi yeh hairan-kun hadd tak dilchasp (surprisingly interesting) bhi hota hai. Pahar khoda chuha nikla (The mountain brought forth a mouse), lekin makkhi (bee) khubsurati aur order (tarteeb) ka ek mo'jiza (miracle) banayegi. Agar unse unke creative process par roshni dalne (enlighten) ko kaha jaye, toh dono sharminda honge, aur shayad unhe koi dilchaspi na ho. Jo artist apne kaam ke so-called meaning par discuss karta hai, wo aam taur par ek adabi pehlu (literary side-issue) ko bayan (describing) kar raha hota hai. Uske asal irade (original impulse) ka nichod (core), agar kahin hai bhi, toh wo khud kaam (work) mein hi milta hai.*

Sculptor Louise Bourgeois, 1954, in *Design Quarterly*

---

## **4 FAILURE (Nakaami/Galti)**

**"Meri classes mein, main is baare mein bahut dhyan rakhti hoon ki fail hone ka kya matlab hai. Iska matlab hai kuch naya try karna aur pehli baar mein sahi nateeja (result) na milna. Students grades par bahut zyada dhyan (oriented) dete hain. Kisi task ko pura kar lene aur waqayi mein kuch sach mein seekhne ke beech ek bahut bada farq hai. Aur sachchi learning (seekh) hone ke liye, kisi na kisi level par failure (nakaami) hona zaroori hai."**

Rhonda Arntsen, graphic design, Savannah College of Art and Design

Creative process mein lagatar failures, surprises, aur accidents hote rehte hain. Failures aisi cheez nahi hain jinse bacha jaye; unhe process ko aage badhane ke mauko (opportunities) ke taur par swagat (welcomed) kiya jana chahiye. Ek "failure" ek lagatar chalne wale creative process mein ek chota aur pehle se umeed kiya jane wala (expected) pal hota hai. Patricia Olynyk, ek artist jo Washington University in St. Louis (WashU) mein alag-alag disciplines (khetron) mein kaam karti hain, unhone mujhe bataya, "Pehla iteration (dohraav) kabhi bhi kaam nahi karta." Failures process ko rokte nahi hain; wo isey aage badhate (drive) hain. Jab failure learning process mein shamil (embedded) hota hai, toh yeh dekhne ki kabiliyat (ability to see) ko paida (fosters) karta hai. Jab aap dekhna seekh jate hain, toh aap jante hain ki kamyabi (successfully) ke sath fail kaise hona hai.

Creativity ambiguity (uljhan) aur exploration (khoj) ki jagah par phalti-phoolti (thrives) hai. Aap yeh jaane bina kaam karna shuru karte hain ki kaun se ideas ubharenge (emerge) ya aakhiri kaam kaise samne aayega (unfold). Wahan hamesha band raste (dead ends) aur nai shuruat (restarts) hongi. Aap aise ideas ki taraf khinche jayenge jo baad mein umeed-bhare (promising) nahi lagenge; aap aise raston (paths) par chalna shuru karenge jo successful kaam ki taraf nahi le jate. Professional creative maharat (expertise) ka matlab failures par aage badhne ki kabiliyat mein maharat hasil karna aur failures ko ek soche-samjhe (deliberate) process mein bun-na (weave) hai jo aakhir-kaar (ultimately) successful kaam ki taraf le jata hai.

**"Apne hathon se kaam karna. Aap kuch karne ki koshish karte hain, aur raste mein aap jo galtiyan (mistakes) karte hain wahi aapke kaam ko anokha (unique) aur alag (different) banati hain. Aur aap uska fayda uthate (capitalize) hain aur aage badhte hain. Yeh kaam karne ka bas ek bahut hi alag tarah ka tareeqa hai."**

Richard Mehl, graphic designer, School of Visual Arts

Agar failure bilkul aakhir mein hoti hai—maan lijiye, jab aap us bees (20) foot unchi murti (sculpture) ke base (buniyad) mein badlaav (modifying) kar rahe hain jis par aapne teen mahine kaam kiya hai, aur wo aapke gallery show ke shuru hone se theek ek din pehle gir (collapses) jati hai—toh isse ubharna (recover) mushkil hota hai. Hum us tarah ke failure ki baat nahi kar rahe hain. Successful artists aur designers ek aise process mein shamil hote hain jahan failures *shuru mein (early)* hote hain. Experiments kaam nahi karte; materials waisa react (respond) nahi karte jaisi aapne umeed ki thi; concepts (ideas) fike pad jate hain (fizzle out) aur bikhre hue (diffuse) lagte hain. Jab aap dekhna jante hain, toh aap in shuruati (early) failures par aage badhte hain aur unse seekhte hain. Aap unhe aisi ghatnaon (events) ke taur par dekhte hain jo creative process ko aage badhate hain. Professors is tarah se padhate hain jisse student ki failure ke bawajood aage badhne ki kabiliyat viksit (develop) hoti hai.

SCAD mein foundations padhane wale painter Stephen Thorpe ne mujhe bataya ki jo students pencil ka istemal karte hain wo pen istemal karne walon se behtar karte hain. Lekin pehle drawing assignment mein, kai students pen ka istemal karke shuruat karte hain. Main unse poochta hoon, agar aap jante hain ki pencil behtar hai, toh aap unhe rokte kyun nahi aur suggest kyun nahi karte ki wo pencil ka istemal karein? "Unhe fail hone dene ke liye." Jo students pen ka istemal karte hain, unhe jaldi hi ehsaas ho jayega ki yeh ek galti (mistake) thi. Thorpe bata sakte hain ki kab kisi student ko yeh ehsaas hota hai ki "Oh yaar, mujhe isey thoda behtar plan karna chahiye tha." Jab student ko yeh niji tajurba (personal experience) hota hai, bajaye iske ki wo professor ki instructions sunein, "Yeh sach mein baat ko dimaag mein bitha deta hai (drives it in)." Tajurba kisi bhi aisi cheez se zyada asardaar (powerful) hota hai jo professor aapko bata sakta hai.

Thorpe ne aage kaha:

*Students ko apne sochne ka tareeqa (model of thinking) badalne ki zaroorat hai. Aapke paas paanch saal ki umar se drawing banane ka jo sochne ka tareeqa hai, wo jab aap bade ho jate hain toh kaam nahi aata (resonate). Yahi wajah hai ki unka fail hona itna zaroori (important) hai. Log waqayi us model ko nahi badalte jab tak ki uska koi nateeja (consequence) na ho. Logon ko girne dena aur phir wapas aane dene ke piche goal (maqsad) yeh hai ki aap hamesha unhe wapas aane ka mauka (opportunity) dete hain.*

Maine WashU mein sculptor Ron Leax dwara padhayi gayi ek studio class visit ki, jinse hum pehli baar introduction mein mile the. Main unke piche piche chal raha tha jab wo kamre mein ghoom rahe the, students ki madad kar rahe the aur unke kaam par discuss kar rahe the. Wo ek student se unke chal rahe (in progress) kaam ke baare mein baat karne ke liye ruke, aur kuch important advice di:

*Isse pehle ki tum aage badho, ek aisi cheez hoti hai jise 'I just screwed myself moment (maine sab kharab kar diya wala pal)' kaha jata hai. Yani, tum ek lambe waqt se kisi cheez par kaam kar rahe ho. Aur yeh bahut badiya chal raha hai. Aur tum aage badh chuke ho aur tumhe pata hai ki yeh 90 percent complete ho chuka hai, lekin tumhe nahi pata, tumhe pata hai ki isme kisi cheez ki kami hai. Sahi kaha na? Aur tum usme shamil hote ho, aur kehte ho "Oh! Lekin agar main yeh karunga, toh yeh isey barbad kar sakta hai!" Pata hai, wo aakhiri 5 percent. Yeh darr hai aur wahin par tumhe bas isey karna hota hai.*

Professors kisi student ke failure ko ek aise tajurbe (experience) mein badalne mein mahir (skilled) hote hain jo unhe dekhna seekhne mein madad karta hai. Class ke baad, Leax ne mujhe bataya: "Aapko gadbad karne (screw up) ki ijazat chahiye. Hum zindagi aur maut (life and death) ka faisla nahi kar rahe hain. Hum bas ajeeb se (stupid) artworks bana rahe hain!" Yahan wo ek aise student se kya kahenge jo bahut zyada conservative (sambhal kar chalne wala/darne wala) ho raha hai: "Main chahta hoon ki tum kuch galtiyan (mistakes) karo. Hamein art school mein failure pasand hai."

Jeffery Keedy, California Institute of the Arts (CalArts) mein padhane wale typeface designer, ne is baare mein baat ki ki an-soche (unexpected) failures ke sath faydemand dhang se (productively) kaam karna seekhna kitna mushkil hai. Jab first-year students program mein dakhil hote hain, toh unhe nahi pata hota ki yeh kaise karna hai; Keedy ka goal unhe sikhana hai. Kabhi-kabhi ek student bure tareeqe se (dramatically) fail hota hai. Unka samna Leax ke "maine sab kharab kar diya (I screwed myself)" wale pal se hota hai aur unhe poori tarah se dobara shuru (restart) karna padta hai. Lekin chauthe saal tak, students ki chhoti failures hoti hain jo bahut zyada specific (khas) hoti hain. Aisa kyun hai ki first-year students ki failures badi hoti hain? Hairani ki baat yeh hai (Paradoxically), aisa isliye hai kyunki wo failure se *bachne (avoid)* ki koshish kar rahe hote hain. Keedy kehte hain:

*Shuruat mein, unhone failure ko process ke ek hisse ke taur par qubool (made peace) nahi kiya hota hai. Wo abhi bhi isse bachne (avoid) ki koshish kar rahe hote hain. Wo abhi bhi wahi karne ki koshish kar rahe hote hain jiske baare mein unhe lagta hai ki hamesha successful hoga. Yahi ek aisi situation paida karta hai jahan wo sirf khud ko dohrate (repeat) hain kyunki unhe samajh aa gaya hai ki kya successful hota hai.*

Ek student kisi ek raste par bahut lambe waqt tak kaam karega kyunki wo apne shuruati idea (initial idea) par bahut zyada focus karta hai; wo abhi bhi kaam karne ke linear process mein phasa hua (invested) hai. Unhone abhi tak iterative exploratory (baar-baar dhoondhne wala) approach nahi seekhi hai. Art aur design school mein, "Wo wahan jitni jaldi ho sake saare galat jawab seekhne ke liye hain."

Yahan tak ki advanced (senior) students ko bhi kabhi-kabhi is baat mein guidance ki zaroorat hoti hai ki failure ka faydemand (productively) istemal kaise kiya jaye. WashU mein ek painter, Lisa Bulawsky, chauthe aur aakhiri saal ke fall semester mein students ke fail hone ki ehmiyat (importance) ke baare mein baat karti hain: "Senior-year ke fall semester ke baare mein meri philosophy yeh hai ki unke paas fail hone ki jitni zyada jagah (room) ho utni honi chahiye, khud se jitna ho sake utna try karne aur ek tarah se apni buniyad (grounded) banane ke liye. Kyunki spring semester unka thesis semester hota hai jab wo ek researched thesis paper likhte hain aur us research ke sath-sath kaam ka ek set (body of work) develop karte hain."

### **Umeed Ki Gayi Galti (The Expected Mistake)**

**Jab tak wo pehle fail nahi hote tab tak wo baat (point) nahi samajhte—unhe ehsaas hota hai ki unhone kya galti (mistake) ki, aur kyun ki. Yeh waqayi important hai.**

John Hendrix, illustrator, Washington University in St. Louis

John Hendrix ek superstar hain. Wo *New York Times* mein publish karte hain; wo magazine covers banate hain; unhone kai kitabein publish ki hain; wo desh ke sabse zyada maang wale (sought-after) illustrators mein se ek hain (dekhein figure 4.1). Hum is baare mein baat karne ke liye baithe ki wo WashU mein first-year foundations kaise padhate hain.

Sew] ‘NORMALIZE STRUGGLE!



**FIGURE 4.1**

John Hendrix. *The Pit of Despair*. Creative process mein failure ka zaroori hona (inevitability) dikhati ek tasveer (graphic representation). 2022.

Sabhi art aur design classes ki tarah, Hendrix sabhi students ko ek jaisa assignment dete hain. Unhe guiding parameters (hidayato/niyamon) ke ek set ke sath ek creative challenge diya jata hai. Challenge open ended hota hai taaki har student apna khud ka hal (solution) nikale, lekin yeh itna structured (tarteeb-war) hota hai ki har student ko ek hi learning outcomes (seekhne ke nateejon) ki taraf guide kiya jata hai. Kyunki wo ek hi creative challenge par kaam kar rahe hote hain aur ek hi creative process ko follow kar rahe hote hain, jab ek student fail hota hai, toh aksar aisa hota hai ki dusre students bhi usi waqt usi tarah se fail hote hain. Jab aap dusre students ko wahi galti karte hue dekhte hain, toh aapko ehsaas hota hai ki yeh aapke baare mein nahi hai. Phir, professor ki guidance ke sath, aap yeh dekhna seekhte hain ki sabhi galtiyon mein kya common (shared) hai aur aap sabne wahi galti kyun ki.

Hendrix ne mujhe bataya ki yeh kaise kaam karta hai, ek poster design karne ke assignment ko describe karke:

*Mera ek pasandeeda project ek cliché (ghisi piti baat) ko lena aur use naya roop (reinvent) dena hai. Wo mere diye gaye phrases (jumlon) mein se chun sakte hain, jaise "Pyaar dard deta hai (Love hurts)" aur "Tum wahi ho jo tum khate ho (You are what you eat)" aur "Kaam par wapas aane se pehle employees ko apne haath dhone chahiye." Wo ek chunte hain aur unhe aisi image banani hoti hai jo iske sath jaye. Lekin image ko cliché (ghisi piti baat) ko thoda palat dena (turn on its head) chahiye.*

Assignment handout ke pehle page ke top par, unhone likha hai, "Is assignment ke sath lafzi (literal) matlab mat nikalna." Pehli class mein, unke kaam shuru karne se pehle, wo unhe dohrate (repeats) hain: "Lafzi (literal) mat bano, bas phrase ko illustrate (tasveer mein dikhana) mat karo." Students phir bhi wahi karte hain—unke pehle solutions hamesha cliché (ghise-pite) hote hain. "Wo hamesha ise bahut lafzi (literal) banate hain, bas phrase ko illustrate karte hain, bhale hi maine unhe aisa na karne ko kaha tha. [Haste hain.] Unhone suna, unhone ise likha, aur phir bhi unhone yahi kiya."

*Phrases mein se ek hai "Kaam par wapas aane se pehle employees ko apne haath dhone chahiye." Ek aam baat (common thing) yeh hai ki log aate hain aur ek aurat dikhate hain, kisi ki image jiske sach mein gande haath (dirty hands) sink (basin) ke paas hon. Jaise mitti wale (muddy), mitti wale haath. Aap bathroom jane se jitni mitti ki umeed karenge usse kahin zyada mitti wale. Lekin yeh us phrase ka lafzi (literal) matlab hai, haina? Yeh asal mein phrase ko illustrate karta hai. Unhe nahi pata ki kisi cheez ko naye andaz me pesh karne (recontextualize) ka sach mein kya matlab hai.*

Hendrix saalon se yeh assignment padha rahe hain. Unke assignment ke pehle step ke baad, andaze ke mutabiq (predictably), wo sabhi cliché (ghisi piti baat) ko recontextualize (naye andaz me pesh karne) ke assignment ko poora karne mein fail ho jate hain. "Maine saalon mein bahut si ek jaisi (similar) cheezein dekhi hain. 'Tum wahi ho jo tum khate ho (You are what you eat)' ke liye, main aksar Mr. Potato Head ko French fries khate hue dekhta hoon." Unhe umeed hai ki aisa hoga aur wo assignment ke agle step ke sath intezar kar rahe hote hain. Yeh step project handout par nahi tha.

*Toh phir, main kehta hoon jao ek magazine dhoondho, bahut saari images scan karo, aur us phrase ko theek uske upar rakh do. Is baare mein socho bhi mat ki iska kya matlab hai. Main unse bees (20) ya tees (30) images try karne ko kehta hoon. Toh unhe ek bhalu (bear) ki picture milti hai, ya kachra phekne wale kisi insaan ki.
