Yeh unhe ek alag tareeqe se un possibilities (sambhavnaon) ko dekhne par majboor karta hai jo ho sakti hain. Agar aap assignment ke shuru mein yeh karte jab aap unhe pehli baar de rahe hote, toh yeh kaam nahi karta. Agar aap kehte "pehli cheez jo main chahta hoon ki aap karein wo hai kisi magazine se ek random image chunein," aur phir wo ek image ke sath aate hain, aur phir aap unhe phrase dete hain. Unhe us failure (nakaami) ki chhoti si deewar ke khilaf dhakka dena padta hai. Unhe use (failure ko) apne sath ek driving force (aage badhane wali taqat) ke taur par rakhna hota hai.*

Students aam taur par phrase ko ek random (kisi bhi) image se jodne (connect) ke qabil hote hain, bhale hi yeh ek bahut hi door ke (distant) aur an-soche (unpredictable) tareeqe se ho. Aur—sabse important baat—random images ke connections lagbhag kabhi bhi lafzi (literal) nahi hote. Is exercise ke baad, Hendrix unhe phrase ko dobara illustrate karne ko kehte hain. Is baar, wo saaf taur par dekh sakte hain; ab wo lafzi (literal) nahi rahe.

*Agar aap unhe yeh dusra assignment shuru mein hi de dete toh yeh kaam nahi karta. Is dusre assignment ke madad karne se pehle unhe us umeed ki gayi nakaami (expected failure) tak pahunchna hoga. Yeh experience karke ki "Maine yeh image banayi aur isne kaam nahi kiya," toh ab mere paas us jagah ki ek sachhi gehri samajh (visceral understanding) hai jahan yeh cheez kaam nahi kar rahi hai. Toh phir, jab wo is random image ko dekh rahe hote hain, toh wo experience theek unke sath wahan hota hai.*

Aap lectures (bhashan) dekar creativity nahi sikha sakte. Agar aap students ko yeh batane ki koshish karte hain ki kya karna hai, toh wo nahi sunenge. Aapko unhe learning process ke zariye guide karna hota hai, ek aesa process jahan wo cliché (ghise-pite) kaam generate karke shuruat karte hain bina yeh jane ki unhone aisa kiya hai, aur aakhir mein, wo apne khud ke kaam mein clichés ko dekhne ke kabil ho jate hain.

Pehli failure ke baad, students agle step mein kamyaab hone ke liye taiyar hote hain. Yahan Hendrix ka non-cliché solution ka ek pasandeeda example hai (dekhein figure 4.2):

*Mera ek pasandeeda solution kuch saal pehle ka tha jab mere student Morgan Schweitzer ne "Pyaar dard deta hai (Love Hurts)" ke liye ek illustration banaya tha. Yeh pani ke andar ek blowfish (ek tarah ki machli) thi jo phooli hui (blown up) thi aur wo ek landmine (zameeni bomb) ko kiss karne wali thi. Khair, ek sea mine (samundari bomb). Aur sea mine phooli hui blowfish jaisi dikh rahi thi. Zahir hai, use sea mine se pyar ho gaya tha. Mere liye, yeh ek be-tahasha (incredibly) creative solution hai kyunki isne kuch aesa liya jise hum sab samajhte hain aur ise bas itna badal diya ki yeh phir se taza (fresh) aur naya ho gaya.*

**FIGURE 4.2** Morgan Schweitzer. *Love Hurts*. Graphite drawing jisme digital color lagaya gaya hai. Washington University in St. Louis mein John Hendrix ke ek assignment ke jawab mein kiya gaya artwork. 2006.

Hendrix dwara is assignment ko bayan (describe) karne ke baad, maine unse poocha, "Kya cheez ise ek acha project banati hai?"

*Mujhe acha lagta hai jab wo is expectation (umeed) ke sath aate hain ki cheez kaisi hone wali hai aur phir unhe ehsaas hota hai ki wo poori tarah se galat (completely off) hain. Project mein sach mein bahut shuru mein, ek pal hota hai, jab wo samajh jate (figuring out) hain ki solution ke baare mein unki pehli soch (assumption) poori tarah se galat hai. Jab aisa hota hai, toh main aksar ek lecture deta hoon jahan main aise kamo ke examples dikhata hoon jo unki ki gayi galti ko darshate (demonstrate) hain. Yeh ek tarah ki umeed ki gayi galti (expected mistake) hoti hai. Aur phir wo isey nayi nazron se dobara dekh (revisit) sakte hain. Aap unhe theek karte hain aur unhe us raste par late hain jahan wo is tareeqe se cheezein bana rahe hote hain ki wo apni progress (tarakki) ko behtar tareeqe se dekhna shuru kar sakte hain.*

Jab shuruaat karne wale students pehli baar kisi assignment par kaam karna shuru karte hain, toh unhe aam taur par turant (right away) idea sochne mein zyada mushkil nahi hoti. Wo us idea par kaam karna shuru kar dete hain aur wo aksar sochte hain ki yahi wo (idea) hai jiske sath wo kaam khatam karenge. Lekin creativity ka yeh linear model (seedha rasta) aam taur par kharab kaam (poor work) ki taraf le jata hai. Zyada-tar shuruaat karne wale students abhi bhi is approach ka istemal kar rahe hote hain aur yahi wajah hai ki unmein se zyada-tar ke paas "umeed ki gayi galti (expected mistake)" hoti hai. Ek acha assignment aese design kiya jata hai jo students ko, unke apne aap par, linear approach ki khamiyon (flaws) ko ehsaas karane ki taraf le jata hai. Jab wo dekhte hain ki unki linear approach ne unhe failure ki taraf le gaya hai, toh wo kaam karne ka ek iterative (baar-baar karne ka) process kisi bhi lecture (bhashan) ke sikhaye jane se kahin behtar seekh lete hain.

*Ek cheez jo hamesha hoti hai wo yeh hai ki wo aate hain aur aap bata sakte hain ki unhe lagta hai ki unhone jo kiya hai wo behtareen (awesome) hai. Aur phir aap point out (ishara) karte hain ki yeh galat kyun hai, aur wo sab kehte hain, "wo sahi hain! Yeh galat hai!" Yeh ek behad keemti (incredibly valuable) tajurba hai. Barah (12) logon ko theek ek hi galti karte hue dekhna, yeh unke liye confirm (pakka) karta hai ki "yeh ek expectation (umeed) hai jo mere paas hai jo wahan bahar maujood hai. Mujhe nahi pata ki yeh kahan se aayi, lekin yeh ek tarah ka natural (qudrati) nateeja hai jo har koi nikalta hai." Community ko ek sath isey experience karte dekh, wo ise kisi personal galti (personal error) ke taur par internalize nahi karte (apne andar nahi bithate). Baarah logon ko wahi galti karte hue dekhna jo aap karte hain, yeh unke liye acha hota hai.*

Unke liye shuru mein ise galat karna faydemand (helpful) kyun hai?

*Ek toh (For one), unke liye yeh na sochna acha hai ki unka kaam keemti (precious) hai. Aap jante hain, art mein yeh ek problem hai, "Main jo kuch bhi banata hoon wo bahut zaroori (important) hai," aur "Meri cheez ko saja kar rakho (Enshrine my thing)." Hum is deewar ko todne ke liye jo kuch bhi kar sakte hain wo madadgar hai. Yeh process mein hamare vishwas (belief), aur kisi cheez ko bahut matra (a lot of) mein banane ki taraf wapas jata hai. Aur perfect ko good (ache) ka dushman (enemy) na banne dena. Agar aap perfect cheez banane ka intezar (hold out) karte rahenge, toh aap wahan tak nahi pahunchenge. Aap un sabhi achi cheezon ko miss kar denge jo aapko perfect cheez tak pahunchne mein madad karti hain. Ya sach mein achi cheez tak.*

Process mein aksar failures aur band raste (dead ends) shamil hote hain. Acha kaam generate karne ke liye an-jani (unknown) cheezon ko explore karne ki zaroorat hoti hai. Hamesha risk hota hai. Students ko risk lena seekhne mein madad karne ke liye, success aur failure ko dekhne ke kabil banne ke liye, aur failure ka faydemand (productively) istemal karne ke liye, instructors aise assignments design karte hain jo students ko ek iterative process ke zariye guide karte hain. Experienced (tajurbakar) professors ne in assignments ka saalon se istemal kiya hai, aur wo apne tajurbe se jante hain ki kai students fail hone wale hain. Wo iski umeed (expecting) kar rahe hote hain aur wo madad karne ke liye taiyar hote hain. Hendrix ki tarah, unka goal (maqsad) students ki is galatfehmi (misconceptions) ko door karna (dislodge) hai ki creativity ek behtareen idea par aadharit (based) hoti hai, ki creative process kisi idea ka ek linear execution (seedhe tareeqe se poora karna) hai. Goal students ko pyaar se (gently) is vishwas se door le jana hai ki unke dwara banaya gaya koi bhi kaam keemti (precious) hai. Ek acha assignment students ko kuch aisa dekhne (see) ki taraf guide karta hai jo wo pehle nahi dekh sakte the.

### **Risks Lene Ki Raza-mandi (The Willingness to Take Risks)**

**Main ek aisi mehfooz jagah (safe place) banana chahta hoon jahan wo buri tarah (miserably) fail ho sakein. Lekin phir aap is sab ko grade (marks) kaise dete hain? Main kehta hoon ki product mayne (matters) nahi rakhta, lekin phir mujhe product ko ek 'A' grade ya kuch dena padta hai. Main kehta hoon, agar aap bahut kadi mehnat (work your asses off) karte hain aur aap buri tarah fail hote hain, toh aapko 'A' milega. Yeh product driven (kisi cheez ke nateeje par zor dene wala) nahi ho sakta; isey process driven (process par zor dene wala) hona chahiye.**

Steve DeFrank, sculptor, School of Visual Arts

School of the Art Institute of Chicago (SAIC) prospective students (aane wale naye students) aur parents se baat karte waqt risk-taking aur failure par zor deta (emphasizes) hai. Maine Tiffany Holmes, ek digital media artist, ka interview liya jab wo SAIC mein thin (ab wo Ringling College of Art and Design mein hain). Unhone mujhe SAIC ke parent orientation presentation ke baare mein bataya, jahan wo "parents ko sach mein is haqeeqat (fact) ke liye taiyar karne ki koshish karte hain ki yeh hamare maqsad (ambitions) mein se ek hai, ki students classroom ki safe space mein failure ko experience karein." Risk lene ki raza-mandi (willingness) zaroori (essential) hai. "Yeh hamare liye ek badi baat (big one) hai. Humne is baare mein bahut bahas (discussions) ki hai ki kisi student ki risk lene ki kabiliyat (capacity) ko kaise judge (evaluate) kiya jaye."

Ron Leax (WashU) ne mujhe bataya ki students theek-thak (reasonably good) kaam karke 'B' grade la sakte hain. Lekin agar wo 'A' grade lana chahte hain, toh unhe 70 ya 80 percent baar fail hona hoga. Aapko experiment karna hoga aur zaahiri (obvious) solution se aage badhna hoga. Beshak, aap deadline se theek pehle fail nahi hona chahte—iska matlab hoga ki aapke paas dikhane ke liye koi kaam nahi hai. Aapko project mein shuru (early) mein fail hone ki zaroorat hai. Yeh un cheezon ko pehchanne (identify) mein aapki madad karta hai jo kaam kar rahi hain. Phir aap unka picha karte (pursue) hain aur unhe develop karte hain, aur process ke aakhir tak, aapke paas kuch aisa hoga jo aala darje (higher level) par kaam kar raha hoga.

### **Gehre Pani Mein Sahara Dena (Extending an Oar in the Deep End)**

Yeh experienced professors hain jinhone saalo se yeh assignments diye hain. Wo jante hain ki students fail honge, lekin wo yeh bhi jante hain ki failure powerful learning (zabarast seekh) ki taraf le jayegi. Wo aam taur par theek se andaza (predict) laga sakte hain ki students process ke kis point par fail honge. Agar failures students ko dekhna seekhne mein madad karte hain, toh wo assignment ko waisa hi rakhenge jaisa wo hai, shayad ise thoda badal (tweak) denge taaki students ko failure ka aur bhi zyada faydemand (productively) istemal karne mein madad mil sake. Studio critiques mein, wo students ko in choti aur umeed ki gayi (expected) failures se seekhne ke liye guide karte hain. SCAD architecture ki professor Julie Varland ne ek behtareen (vivid) misaal (metaphor) ka istemal kiya:

*Main aam taur par ek aise mahoul ki himayat karti (espouse) hoon jo unhe gehre pani (deep water) mein fek deta hai. Aur main unke sath wahin intezar kar rahi hoti hoon, ek tarah se ek naav (boat) mein [hasti hain], jab unhe zaroorat hoti hai toh unhe oar (chappu/sahara) deti (extending) hoon. Bina risk ke, aap nahi seekhte. Aur aap bas bahut hi tay shuda tareeqe (highly prescribed way) se cheezein kar rahe hote hain, aap hamesha safe khel rahe (playing it safe) hote hain. Toh jab main unhe gehre pani (deep end) mein fekti hoon, toh main unhe bas wahan chhod nahi deti. Unke liye bahut support hota hai.*

SCAD ke ek sculptor ne lagbhag yahi misaal (metaphor) istemal ki: "Mujhe lagta hai ki kisi insaan ko nadi mein phekna aur unhe yeh dhoondhne (figure out) par majboor karna zaroori hai ki zinda kaise bachen (survive). Main aise hi padhata hoon. Main positively unka support karna jaari rakhta hoon aur main yeh pakka (make sure) karta hoon ki wo aakhir mein fail na hon, wo semester ke is sach mein mushkil (demanding) pehle hisse ke beech mein (midst) kamyabi (success) ko experience karein. Dusre hisse mein, hum analyze (jaanch) karte hain ki unhone tairte rehne (stay afloat) ke liye kya kiya." Failure ko learning experience mein badalne ke liye is par gaur karna (Reflecting) zaroori (required) hai. Wo aage kehte hain: "Yeh ek professional designer ya artist ban-ne ka raaz (secret) hai. Yeh aam (intuitive) creativity se soche-samjhe (deliberate) creativity mein badalne par majboor (forces) karta hai. Kamyaab (succeed) hone ke liye aapko ek soche-samjhe (deliberate) creator banna hoga. Hum logon ko professional creative minds ban-ne ki training dete hain."

Jaise-jaise students apne projects par kaam karte hain, wo un chhote tareeqon se fail hote hain jo unke samajh ke level ke hisab se theek (appropriate) hote hain. Professors umeed kar rahe hote hain ki students fail honge, aur jab wo fail hote hain, toh wo students ko failure se seekhne ke liye guide karne ke liye taiyar (prepared) hote hain. Jeff Pike (WashU) ne kaha, "Ek acha instructor ambiguity (uljhan) ke masle (issue) ko aamne-samne se nipatta (frontal assault) hai aur ek tarah se unhe pool ke gehre hisse mein fek deta hai. Aur phir unhe guide karta hai." Jab koi student fail hota hai, toh instructors unse baat karte hain ki aisa kyun hua. Yeh failure ka apne creative process mein istemal karne ki student ki kabiliyat ko badhata (increases) hai.

**Yeh kisi hawai jahaz (airplane) ke haadse (disaster) ke black box ki tarah hai. Aapko yeh samajhne ke liye black box mein recording ko analyze karna hota hai ki plane crash kyun hua. Main students se kehti hoon, hamein apne artwork ke sath bhi wahi karne ki zaroorat hai. Agar koi cheez kaam nahi kar rahi hai, successful nahi hai, toh aakhir kyun? Main unse kehti hoon, us haadse (disaster) ko lekar aao aur phir hum iske baare mein baat karte hain. Isse wo thoda aaram (relaxes) mehsoos karte hain.**

Amy Honchell, ek painter aur sculptor SAIC mein

Jab maine Rhode Island School of Design (RISD) aur School of Visual Arts (SVA) ki Kara Rooney se baat ki, toh maine unhe bataya ki kai professors ne students ko gehre pani mein doobne aur bachaye (rescued) jane ki misaal (metaphor) ka istemal kiya tha. Wo turant samajh gayin ki unka kya matlab tha aur unhone haan mein sir hilaya (nodded affirmation). Lekin unhone ise thoda clear (clarified) kiya: "Main unka kinaray se (from the shore) hosla badhati (cheering) hoon. Main utni madad nahi karti (less hands on). Agar unhe kuch der struggle (jaddo-jehad) karne ki zaroorat hai, toh unhe struggle karna chahiye. Main bahut zyada unglia pakad kar chalane (handholding) mein yakin nahi rakhti. Mustaqbil (future) mein studio mein aapke liye aisa karne wala koi nahi hoga." Yeh karna mushkil ho sakta hai. Asal mein professor ke liye student ko struggle karne dena zyada mushkil (more work) hota hai. "Mere liye unhe yeh batana zyada aasan hai ki jis gaddhe (hole) mein wo hain usse bahar kaise nikle, bajaye iske ki main unhe us gaddhe se bahar nikalne ke liye struggle karte hue dekhu. Lekin unhe yeh khud ke liye karna hoga."

Is balance ko theek (right) karna bahut mushkil hai. Isme saalo ka teaching experience lagta hai. Ek professor ke support ke bina, students ko failure ka fayda (benefit) nahi milta. Lekin bahut zyada support hone par, students ko asli (genuine) failure ko experience karne ka mauka nahi milta. Expert teachers jante hain ki kya karna hai, lekin kisi student ko isse guzarte hue dekhna kabhi aasan nahi hota. Aapki instinct (aadat) hoti hai ki aap beech mein kood jayein (step in) aur student ki zyada madad karein.

Toh, aap unhe isse pehle ki aap beech mein aayein (step in) aur unhe yeh ehsaas dilaein ki unka rasta kaam nahi karne wala hai, kahan tak jane dete hain? Kuch professors unhe bhatakne (flounder) dete hain aur khud hi rasta nikalne (figure it out) dete hain. Dusre professors jaldi hi (earlier) beech mein kood jate hain. Accra Shepp, ek photographer jo SVA mein padhate hain, unhone mujhe bataya ki 1988 mein jab unhone padhana shuru kiya tha tab se unka nazariya (view) badal gaya tha: "Jab maine padhana shuru kiya, toh maine socha, shayad student ko fail hone dena aur phir kuch naya batana (introduce) zaroori hai. Lekin ab mera nazariya yeh hai, chalo seedhi baat karte hain (let's cut to the chase). Main unhe khayi (cliff) ki taraf chalte hue dekh sakta hoon. Shayad mujhe unhe khayi se girne se rokna chahiye. Main logon ko ek tay khatre (certain peril) mein khud ko phekne nahi deta. Zyada-tar waqt, unhe ehsaas ho jata hai ki meri advice waqayi kaam kar rahi hai." Yeh faisle (judgments) lena padhane ki art (kala) ka ek hissa hai.

### **Faydemand Nakaami (Productive Failure)**

**"Main faydemand failures (productive uses of failure) ko pukhta tor par man-ne wali (firm believer) hoon. Mujhe lagta hai ki failure creative process ka ek bahut bada hissa hai jiske sath students ko sahaj (comfortable) hona seekhna chahiye aur yeh samajhna chahiye ki, aap jante hain, kabhi-kabhi aapko us cheez ko dhoondhne ke liye kachra (trash) bahar nikalna padta hai jo iske niche dabi (buried) thi."**

Amy Honchell, painter aur sculptor, SAIC

Kuch naye dhang ki (innovative) science classes ek teaching method ka istemal karti hain jise *productive failure* (faydemand nakaami) kaha jata hai, jise gehri (deeper) learning ka nateeja (result) dene ke liye sabit (proven) kiya ja chuka hai. Yeh classes students ko open-ended (bina tay shuda jawab wale) challenges deti hain, jinke kai mumkin hal (possible solutions) hote hain, jahan students aksar shuru mein galat rasta chun lete hain. Teacher ki guidance (rehnumai) se, wo sahi hal ko samajhna shuru karte hain, aur sath hi sath, wo dheere-dheere samajhte hain ki wo fail kyun hue. Is tarah, wo sirf sahi jawabon ko yaad (memorize) nahi karte; wo buniyaadi science principles (usoolon) ko gehrai se samajhte hain. Yeh science projects art aur design assignments ke sath kai khasiyate (characteristics) share karte hain. Pehla, unke kai mumkin solutions hote hain. Dusra, unme aisi constraints (rukawatein) hoti hain jo students ko un strategies ka istemal karne se rokti hain jinme unhone pehle hi maharat (mastery) hasil kar li hai. Students ko aisi nayi strategies ke sath experiment karna padta hai jo unki mojuda (current) kabiliyat ke level se aage (beyond) ki hon. Teesra, assignment ki yeh maang hoti hai ki students kisi ek idea ko aage badhane (pursue) ke liye chun-ne se pehle kai ideas pesh (propose) karein. Jab students aage badhne ke liye possible (mumkin) raston (paths) ki ek badi range banate (generate) hain, toh wo zyada gehrai se (deeply) seekhte hain.

Productive failure projects mein open-ended challenges ko us behtareen jagah (sweet spot) ko dhoondhne ke liye *calibrated (naap-tol kar set kiya gaya)* hona chahiye jahan students ko bas kafi (just enough) challenge kiya jaye, lekin wo pareshan (frustrated) na hon. Art aur design professors bhi yahi karte hain—wo lagatar apne assignment design ko calibrate karte hain. Wo bata sakte hain ki kab project parameters (niyam) bahut zyada rukawat wale (constraining) hain kyunki students ek linear process (seedha rasta) ko follow karte hain, koi iteration (dohraav) nahi hota, aur koi fail nahi hota. Iske ulat (Inversely), jab parameters bahut zyada open-ended hote hain, toh students ko nahi pata hota ki shuruat kahan se karein, process ruk jata (stalls) hai, aur wo us point tak bhi nahi pahunch pate jahan wo fail hon. Wo gair-faydemand (unproductively) tareeqe se bhatakte (flounder) hain. Ek acha professor assignment ki design ko in do imtehao (extremes) ke beech mein se guzaarna (steer) seekh jata hai. Is kitab mein aap jin assignments ke baare mein padhte hain, unhe saalon ke classroom istemal ke zariye calibrate kiya gaya hai.

Jab koi student fail hone ke darr se sunn (paralyzed) ho jata hai, toh SAIC mein ek painter Candida Alvarez unse kehti hain: "Sabse buri (worst) painting banao jo tum soch sakte ho ki tum abhi mere liye bana sakte ho. Bas isey banao." Student tab jaan jata hai ki professor failure (nakaami) ki umeed (expecting) kar rahi hain. Kamyaab hone (succeed) ka koi pressure nahi hai.

SVA ke Steve DeFrank bhi kuch aisa hi karte hain. Wo pehle saal ke dusre semester mein ek assignment dete hain: "Students ko ek poori tarah se (utter) failure banane mein do hafte bitane padte hain. Unka goal fail hona hai. Students hamesha poochte hain, khair, kya hoga agar main kuch aesa banau jo sach mein khubsurat (beautiful) ho? [Haste hain.] Aur main kehta hoon, khair, tum fail ho gaye. Aur isliye tumhe ek 'A' milta hai. Phir wo kehte hain, kya hoga agar main bas kuch sach mein ghatiya (crappy) karu? Aur main kehta hoon, tumne wahi kiya jo maine manga tha, tumhe ek 'A' milta hai."

Professors productive failures—wo jo students ko creative process mein maharat (master) hasil karna seekhne mein madad karti hain—aur aisi failures jo madadgar nahi (not helpful) hoti hain, unke beech farq (distinguish) karte hain. Rhonda Arntsen (graphic design, SCAD) ne mujhe bataya, "Main failure ko badhava (encourage) deti hoon. Mujhe lagta hai ki yeh creativity sikhata hai aur students ko apni creativity dhoondhne (discover) mein madad karta hai. Main ek aesa mahoul (environment) banane ki koshish karti hoon jo jigyasa (curiosity) ka ek level paida (inspires) kare." Wo apne projects ko is tarah design karti hain ki students shuru (early) mein hi experiment karein aur unki failures shuruat (beginning) mein aayein. "Failure ka matlab aakhiri nateeje (end outcome) se nahi hai. Aksar (Quite often), aap jante hain, zyada tar (more often) wo kamyaab (successful) hote hain, kyunki wo failure us tehqeeqat (investigation) mein pehle hi ho chuka hai. Aur un cheezon ke pehle kuch hafte aksar bilkul (absolutely) bhayanak (awful) ho sakte hain, jahan students ke liye multiple (kai) failures hote hain."

Science mein productive failure projects mein, students ko ek openended problem di jati hai jise is tarah design kiya jata hai ki students galat raste (wrong path) par jayein aur ek band raste (dead end) tak pahunchein. Phir unhe sahi hal (correct solution) bataya jata hai, jiske baad class mein aisi baatcheet (conversation) hoti hai jo unhe unki failure ki wajah (nature) ko samajhne mein madad karti hai. Art aur design assignments math aur science projects ke sath kai baatein share karte hain. Dono mein, student ka failure tab zyada faydemand (productive) hota hai jab yeh process ke shuru (early) mein hota hai. Science subjects mein productive failure ki tarah, jab aap ek art aur design assignment ke dauran raste mein (along the way) fail hote hain, toh aap ek gehri samajh (deeper understanding), dekhne ki kabiliyat (ability to see) viksit (develop) karte hain.

Science project ke ulat (Unlike), ek art ya design assignment ka koi ek sahi solution (hal) nahi hota. Har student process ke zariye apna khud ka creative rasta apnata hai, aur har student ka banaya hua (creation) dusron se alag hota hai. Jab students aage rasta band dekh kar ruk jate hain (reach an impasse), toh professors students ko yeh nahi batate ki aage kya karna hai. Iske bajaye, wo students ko apne khud ke decisions (faisle) lene ke liye guide karte hain, aur wo students ko yeh samajhne mein madad karte hain ki aese decisions kaise liye jayen.

Ek aur farq yeh hai ki science mein productive failure projects ke sath, failure aam taur par ek problem ko solve karte waqt ek hi baar hota hai, jiske baad teacher sahi solution present (pesh) karta hai. Iske bar-aks (In contrast), art aur design mein, jab koi student kisi project par kaam karta hai toh failure ke multiple moments (kai pal) ho sakte hain. Har student ka alag ginti (number) mein failures se samna (encounter) ho sakta hai. Jab students fail hote hain, toh iska matlab yeh nahi hai ki wo kisi galat jawab (incorrect answer) par pahunche hain. Balki (Rather), failure ek lambe (extended) creative process mein ek waqti jhatka (temporary blip) hai, aur multiple moments of failure aksar is lambe creative process mein hote hain, jisme se har ek seekhne (learning) mein madad (contributing) karta hai.

Ambiguity (Uljhan) aur frustration (niraasha) ke samne, ek risk yeh hai ki seekhne wale (learners) bahut jaldi haar maan lete (give up) hain. Agar learners bahut jaldi haar maan lete hain, toh productive failure designs kaam nahi karte. Open-ended assignments ke samne, learners ko datay rehne (persistence) aur lagan (tenacity) ki zaroorat hoti hai, kaam mein lage rehne (engage in) ki khwahish bhale hi wo struggle aur fail ho rahe hon. Art aur design college ke students bahut motivated hote hain, aur wo aam taur par apni failure aur frustration (pareshani) ke zariye kaam karte hue datay rehne (persistence) ka ek high level (uncha star) dikhate (demonstrate) hain. Phir bhi, failure dil todne wala (demoralizing) ho sakta hai, aur yeh professors students ko failure ke experience ke sath comfortable (sahaj) hone mein guide karne, yeh samjhane mein ki yeh zaroori (necessary) kyun hai, aur yeh creative process mein maharat (master) hasil karne mein unki kaise madad karega, isme unke sath one-on-one (ek-akela) bahut saara waqt (substantial time) bitate hain.

### **Failure ko Saaf Taur par Dekhna (Seeing Failure Clearly)**

Ek science class mein productive failure designs mein, student ko zaroori nahi ki pata ho ki uska jawab galat (wrong) hai jab tak ki instructor (teacher) ise point out (ishara) na kare. Phir bhi, unhe abhi tak yeh nahi pata hoga ki yeh galat *kyun (why)* hai. Yeh baat art school ke pehle saalo mein students ke liye bhi sach hai. Professors students ko yeh ehsaas (realize) karane mein guide karte hain ki wo jo kar rahe hain wo kaam nahi kar raha hai. Lekin student wo nahi dekh sakta jo professors dekhte hain. Yahan tak ki jab kisi student ko pata hota hai ki kuch poori tarah theek nahi hai, tab bhi wo yeh nahi samajhte ki wo is failure wale pal tak kyun pahunche hain. Wo nahi jante ki apne aage chalne wale creative process mein iska faydemand (productively) istemal kaise karna hai. Studio classes mein, art aur design professors students ko apni failures ko jaanchne (examine), wo kyun fail huye iske liye wajah (explanations) dhoondhne, aur is baat ko explore (khoj) karne ke liye encourage karte hain ki wo failure se aage kaise badh (build on) sakte hain.

Failures process ko aage badhati (drive) hain. Wo wahi kaam (function) karti hain jo ek aesa accident (haadsa) karta hai jo shayad creative process ke bahar se aaye. Chapter 1, "Process" mein, maine Lauren Cardenas ki kahani sunayi thi ki kaise usne ampules—sealed glass capsules—ka ek dabba (case) mangwaya (ordered) tha, lekin unke top (upar ka hissa) gayab (missing) the. Unhe phekne (throw away) ke bajaye, unhone us problem ko ek mauke (opportunity) ke taur par istemal kiya taaki wo apne idea ko ek nayi disha (direction) mein le ja sakein. Unhone isey ek "khushgawar itefaq (happy accident)" kaha; yeh ek aesi cheez hai jise aap kisi aur cheez ya insaan ka intezar karne ke bajaye, khud ke liye hone de sakte hain (make happen). Aap aisa lagatar experimenting (nayi cheezein try) karke karte hain, materials ko aapko lead (raasta dikhane) karne dete hain, aur phir jab kuch dilchasp (interesting) hota hai toh usay dekhne (seeing) ke liye open (khule) rehte hain.

Jab koi rasta ek dead end (band rasta) par pahunch jata hai, toh yeh sirf raye (opinion) ki baat nahi hoti. Ek "dead end" ka matlab yeh nahi hai ki aapko bas iski dikhawat (looks like) pasand nahi aayi aur aapne kuch aur karne ka faisla kiya. Iska matlab yeh nahi hai ki agar aapne thodi aur koshish (tried harder) ki hoti toh aap isey bacha (salvage) lete. Artists dead end ke baare mein aise baat karte hain jaise yeh ek *asli cheez (real thing)* ho, unhone jo kiya hai uske baare mein ek objective truth (vishwawyapi sach). Ek dead end ek haqeeqat (reality) hai; aap bas us raste par aage *nahi (cannot)* badh sakte. Ek baar jab aap ise saaf (clearly) dekh lete hain, toh aap isse inkar (deny) nahi kar sakte. Yahi wajah hai ki dead ends ko saaf taur par dekh pane ke kabil hona behad zaroori (critical) hai—kyunki warna aap deewar par apna sir patakte (banging) rahenge.

Jab aap dekhna jante hain, toh aap jante hain ki kab is raste (path) ko chhod kar kisi dusre raste par jane ka waqt aa gaya hai. Professional artists aur designers process mein dead ends aur mistakes (galtiyon) ki umeed (expect) karna jante hain; wo in palon ko pehchan (recognize) sakte hain. Wo in palon par dhyaan (reflect) dete hain, is baat par ki us point tak unka process kis tarah dead end tak lekar gaya, aur yeh process mein us point par kyun hua. Wo in gaur-o-fikar (reflections) ka istemal umeed-bhare (promising) naye creative raston ko aage (forward) pehchan-ne mein apni madad ke liye karte hain.

### **Abhi Ke Liye, Yeh Kaam Karta Hua Lagta Hai, Lekin (Right Now, It Seems to Work, but)**

Hum Joe DiGioia ki 3D design ki class mein hain, aur students sustainable (lambe arse tak chalne wale) product design par ek project assignment kar rahe hain. Wo ek critique (tajziya) kar rahe hain aur ek student ka mota (rough) prototype (shuruati model) pakde hue hain. Wo dono isey dekh rahe hain jab wo ise ghuma rahe (turns it around) hain, iski jaanch (examines) kar rahe hain, aur out loud (zor se) soch rahe hain. Giniye ki wo kitni baar *abhi ke liye (right now)* kehte hain:

* 1 Ek hi cheez jo mujhe isme thodi pareshan karti (bothers) hai
* 2 wo is plastic wali cheez ka istemal hai *abhi ke liye (right now)*.
* 3 Mera matlab hai, mujhe lagta hai ki yeh—tum jante ho,
* 4 *abhi ke liye (right now)* yeh kaam karta hua lagta hai.
* 5 Yeh theek (fine) kaam karta hai. Tum jante ho.
* 6 Yeh isey us tarah se seal (band) karta hai jaise ise hona chahiye.
* 7 Sahi (Right), Yeh tight (kasa hua) hai, um . . .
* 8 yeh nami (moisture) ko bahar rakhega, jo bahut acha (great) hai,
* 9 Toh, (3) um . . . (1)
* 10 [Jeff prototype ko niche rakh deta hai]
* 11 Mujhe nahi pata.
* 12 Mujhe lagta hai ki iska istemal karne ka koi na koi tareeqa hona chahiye, uh,
* 13 ek sakht (rigid) paper product, um. (2)
* 14 Lekin dekho *abhi ke liye (right now)*
* 15 hum dono ka istemal karne ki baat kar rahe hain,
* 16 iske dono taraf (sides).

Wo teen baar *abhi ke liye (right now)* (lines 2, 4, aur 14) lafz (phrase) ka istemal karte hain. Dhyan se sunein jis tarah se wo *abhi ke liye (right now)* kehte hain; wo ishaara kar rahe hain (implying) ki student ke kaam ki mojooda halat (current state) paidaar nahi (unstable) hai. Yeh rasta (path) aage nahi ja sakta; koi aur disha (direction) tay karni padegi. Wo kaam mein kuch aesa dekhte hain jo student abhi tak nahi dekh paa rahi hai, aur wo student ko wo dekhne ke liye guide kar rahe hain jo wo abhi tak nahi dekh payi hai. Gaur karein ki wo student ko yeh nahi *batate (tell)* ki masala (issue) kya hai aur wo student ko yeh nahi *batate* ki use kya karna chahiye. In teeno cases mein, *abhi ke liye (right now)* kehne ke baad, wo kaam ke baare mein kuch positive (acha) bayan karte hain, jabki sath hi yeh bhi ishara karte hain ki kuch badalne ki zaroorat hai. Lekin student ko problem batane ke bajaye, wo student ko khud (on her own) us problem ko dhoondhne (discovering) ki taraf le jate hain.

Maine bahut se professors ko is tarah se *abhi ke liye (right now)* ka istemal karte dekha hai. *Abhi ke liye (right now)* ka istemal is tarah se kiya jata hai jo student ki samajh ke unke maujooda (current) level par milta hai, is baat ko tasleem (acknowledging) karte huye ki yeh theek (okay) hai—yahan tak ki yeh umeed (expected) bhi ki jati hai—ki student abhi tak masle (issue) ko nahi dekh pa raha hai. *Abhi ke liye (right now)* ka istemal yeh kehne ka ek aasan sa (indirect) tareeqa hai ki student apne khud ke kaam mein issue ko nahi dekh pa raha hai, jabki student ko yeh bhi batana hai ki yeh unki taraf se koi kami (failing) nahi hai. Yeh message deta hai ki dead ends (band raste) aur uncertainty (ghair yakeeni/dubdha) process ka ek aam (normal) hissa hain. Ek tarah se, student ne koi mistake (galti) *nahi (not)* ki hai, balki ek mistake ka *samna (encountered)* kiya hai, ek aesa design faisla (decision) liya hai jo sirf piche mud kar dekhne (retrospect) par hi ek dead end (band raste) ke roop mein dikhai deta hai.

*Abhi ke liye (Right now)* ka message yeh hai ki hum ek aage chalne wale (ongoing) process ke ek waqti (temporary) pal par hain. Creativity ek iterative (doohraya jane wala) process hai; aage badhne ka behtareen rasta tab tak nahi jana ja sakta jab tak ki pehla qadam (prior action) na uthaya jaye; aur aksar pehla qadam ek dead end ki taraf le jata hai. Creative kaam mukammal (finished) nahi hua hai; abhi bhi ek andaruni kashmakash (internal tension) baqi hai. Failure ki theek wajah (nature) abhi tak samne nahi (revealed itself) aayi hai. DiGioia isay dekh sakte hain, lekin wo student ko nahi batate ki wo kya dekhte hain; wo chahte hain ki wo khud ise dhoondh (figure it out) nikale. Jaise-jaise student kaam jaari rakhegi, failure ki specific details khud ba khud samne aa jayengi. Student ko bas saaf nigah (clear vision) aur aagahi (awareness) ke sath kaam karte rehna hai. Professor aasan taur par (indirectly) yeh communicate (batate) karte hain ki kaam ki haqeeqat (reality) aur student ke kaam ko dekhne ke nazariye (perception) ke beech ka yeh mismatch creative process ka ek aam aur expected (jiski umeed ki jati ho) hissa hai.

### **Brownies Laao (Bring Brownies)**

**Main students ko janti hoon. Main hafte ke har din yahan hoti hoon, weekends aur raaton (nights) mein bhi. Main unke liye yahan hoon. Students bahut stressed (tanaav) mein aa jate hain. Kabhi-kabhi mujhe lagta hai ki main yahan sirf unhe yeh batane ke liye hoon ki main unke liye yahan hoon.**

Catalina Freixas, architecture, Washington University in St. Louis

Har educator chahta hai ki uske students kamyaab (succeed) hon. Art aur design professors isliye failure ko badhava (encouraging) nahi de rahe hain kyunki unhe parwah (uncaring) nahi hai. Koi nahi chahta ki yeh ek boot camp (sakht military training) ho. Wo is maqsad (goal) se assignments design nahi karte ki students fail honge; lekin wo jante hain ki jab koi assignment students ko dekhna seekhne mein achi tarah kaam karta hai, toh aesa hone ki sambhavna (likely) hai ki students raste mein fail honge.

Kisi ko bhi fail hona pasand nahi hai. Students ek productive dead end (faydemand band raste) ko apni niji nakami (personal failing) ke roop mein internalize (apne andar lene) kar sakte hain. Wo ek ambiguous (ghair wazeh/jiski theek se samajh na aaye) assignment dene ya apni umeedon (expectations) ko saaf (clear) na karne ke liye professor ko dosh (blame) de sakte hain. Jab assignments achi tarah design kiye jate hain, toh yeh lazmi (inevitable) failures students ko yeh samajhne mein madad karte hain ki creative process ke zariye kamyaab (successful) kaise hona hai. Professional artists aur designers hamesha fail hote hain. Jab students graduate honge aur creative professionals banenge, toh wo apne poore creative careers mein—in productive (faydemand) tareeqon se—fail honge. *Kabhi* bhi aesa waqt nahi aayega jab aap itne talented honge ki aap ab fail nahi honge. Professors students ko fail hone se *bachna (avoid)* nahi sikha rahe hain; wo unhe kamyabi (successfully) se fail hona sikha rahe hain. Catalina Freixas yeh janti hain. "Yeh bahut stressful hai. Students bahut stressed (tanaav) mein aa jate hain." Isliye wo brownies banati hain. "Main unhe rishwat (bribe) deti hoon. Main kehti hoon, 'Aap Monday ke liye apna model finish karein, aur main brownies laungi.' Aur main sach mein brownies lati hoon. Log aise hote hain, 'Aap apne students ke liye khana (cook) kyun banati hain?' Aap jante hain, main unki family hoon aur wo meri family hain. Hum ek sath itna waqt guzarte hain!" Main hasta hoon aur unse poochta hoon, kya aapko sach mein brownie nahi milti agar aap khatam (finish) nahi karte? Wo hasti hain aur mazaaq mein (jokingly) tasavvur karti hain ki wo kya keh sakti hain: "Tumhare liye koi brownie nahi. Yeh sach hai! Model finish karo, tumhe ek brownie milegi. Main Halloween ke liye candies lati hoon, Valentine's Day ke liye candies lati hoon."

### **Grades (Marks) ke Sath Problem**

Mere interviews top universities mein the. Inme admission (get in) pane ke liye, aapko ek acha student hona chahiye. Unhe ache grades (marks) aane ki aadat hoti hai. Undergraduate BFA students ke sath, college ke liye unka approach (rawaiya) waisa hi hota hai jaisa high school mein tha: pata lagao (find out) ki teacher kya chahta hai, seekho ki A grade lane ke liye kya karna hai, aur phir apna poora zor (effort) us A ko lane par lagao. Lekin yeh approach creativity ko poori tarah block kar deta hai. Beth Grabowski Chapel Hill (UNC) mein University of North Carolina mein hain, jo United States ki panchvi (fifth) rank wali state university hai. UNC mein ek art department hai lekin wahan art school nahi hai; art students bhi ussi application process se guzarte hain aur wahi zaroori (required) courses lete hain jo baaki sabhi (non-art) majors lete hain. Yeh students na sirf ache artists hain; unhone sabhi subjects mein badiya karkardagi (excelled) dikhayi hai. Unke test scores top par hote hain aur wo school mein acha karne ke liye motivated (prerit) hote hain. Grabowski ne mujhe bataya:

*UNC mein, in students ko apni zindagi mein A's milne ki aadat rahi hai aur har cheez ko ek acha student hone ki is criteria (metric) par napa jata hai. Raste mein (along the way) fail hona ya kuch try karna aur phir buri tarah se fail hona (falling flat on your face) unke liye option (vikalp) hi nahi hai. Aur unhe isse ubharna hoga (get over that) kyunki kahin bhi pahunchne ke liye aapko waqayi mein fail hone ke liye aur ek bade (dramatic) tareeqe se fail hone ke liye taiyar (willing) rehna hoga. Main kabhi-kabhi apne students se kehti hoon ki mujhe apni failures dikhayein aur main unhe saaf taur par (up front) batati hoon ki main umeed karti hoon ki wo kamyab (succeed) hone se pehle fail honge. Main kam se kam yeh khayal unke dimaag mein dalti (plant) hoon ki main umeed nahi karti ki yeh pehli baar mein hi perfectly (poori tarah se sahi) aayega. Main unhe yeh bhi batati hoon ki yeh unke time management ka hissa hai. Unhe is haqeeqat (fact) ki umeed rakhni (anticipate) hogi ki wo shuruat mein hi (right out of the gate) kamyab (succeed) nahi honge.*

Dusri college classes ke ulat (Unlike), students andaza (predict) nahi laga sakte ki wo kab free (done) honge. Iska matlab hai ki wo assignment due (jama karne ka waqt) hone ki aakhiri raat tak intezar nahi kar sakte; ek an-socha (unexpected) failure unse deadline miss karwa dega.

Ek baar jab students fail hone se darna band karna seekh jate hain, toh wo behtar kaam karna shuru kar dete hain. RISD ke Anther Kiley ne mujhe bataya: "Problem tab hoti hai jab students koodne (jump in) aur kuch kharab (screw something up) karne se darte hain. Aam taur par wahi students isse zyada seekhte (get more out of it) hain jo cheezon ko kharab karte hain."

### **Yeh Zindagi ya Maut Nahi Hai; Yeh Art Hai (It's Not Life or Death; It's Art)**

Candida Alvarez (SAIC) ko "failure" shabd pasand nahi hai. Jab main unse poochta hoon ki jab koi student ek dead end par pahunch jata hai toh wo kya karti hain, wo kehti hain "wo aam taur par tab hota hai jab wo kisi problem ko pechida (complicate) kar dete hain, jab wo situation mein bahut zyada cheezein (variables) daal dete hain. Yeh failing nahi hai. Main kahungi ki idea ko aasan (simplify) banao. Main bas itna kahungi ki jao aur zyada experiment karo."

**Agar aap ek A (grade) pana chahte hain, toh aapko 70 percent baar fail hona hoga. Unhe apne ideas ko aage badhana (push) hoga. Unhe experiment karna hoga.**

SCAD mein ek sculpture professor

Jess Parris Westbrook (DePaul) ko failure ki is language par sakht aitraaz (strongly objects) hai:

*Mujhe "failure" shabd se sakht nafrat (absolutely abhor) hai. Aur mujhe "fail" shabd se nafrat hai. Main asal mein iska kabhi istemal nahi karti. Mujhe nahi lagta ki us shabd ke sath kuch bhi constructive (taameri/faydemand) ya productive juda hai. Asal mein real (asli) failures hoti hain, aur shayad art classroom mein sabse safe failures ho sakti hain. Lekin wo shabd, "failure," itna trending (charcha mein) hai. "Failure" shabd mein haqeeqat (reality) aur gambhirta (gravity) hai, aur yeh mere liye zindagi aur maut (life and death) jaisa hai. Isliye, agar main isey kisi process se jodti (assign) hoon, toh yeh lagbhag bewaqoofi (silly) lagta hai. Yeh zindagi ya maut nahi hai. Main isse dur rehti hoon. Isme ek ajeeb sa privileged quality (khaas logo wala ehsas) hai. Isey aksar un business/startup (entrepreneurial) type situations mein istemal kiya jata hai jinke paas ek safety net (bachav ka jaal) hota hai jahan real (asli) failure ho sakti hai. Lekin is planet par zyada-tar log ek real failure nahi hone de sakte. Yeh tabah-kun (devastating) hoga.*

Kabhi-kabhi, jise students ek failure mante (perceive) hain, wo darasal bas ek mismatch (farq) hota hai is baat mein ki unhe kya lag raha tha ki wo kya kar rahe hain aur kaam karte waqt asal mein kya nikal kar aaya (came out) (hum isey agle chapter, "Seeing" mein aur zyada detail mein dekhenge (explore)). Wo an-sochi cheez jo hui, jise wo ek galti mante hain, darasal aage badhne ka ek behtar rasta sabit ho sakti (turn out) hai.

Phir professors students ko yeh dekhne mein madad karenge ki unhone kya kiya hai, iska potential (kabiliyat/dam) dekhein, aur aage badhne ka naya rasta dekhein.

An-sochi (Unexpected) cheezein samne (emerge) aati hain. Artists aur designers isey failure ke taur par nahi sochte. Agar aap ek aakhiri (final) kaam banane ka pukhta irada (determined) rakhte hain jo aapke shuruati (original) irade se mel khata (matches) ho, aur kuch aesa nikal aata hai jo aapke irade ke khilaf (contrary) hai, toh haan, aap apne shuruati irade ko sach sabit (realize) karne mein fail hue hain. Lekin successful artists aur designers use ek failure ke taur par nahi dekhte. Students ise sirf isliye ek failure ke taur par experience (mehsoos) karte hain kyunki wo abhi tak us cheez ka potential (kabiliyat) nahi dekh paate jo ubhar kar (emerged) aayi hai. Creative process mein, surprises honge. Aap kaam ki sun-na (listen) aur direction badalna seekhte hain. Failures aapko batati hain ki kahan jana hai.

---

#### CHAPTER 5

## SEEING (Dekhna)

**"Kabhi-kabhi students ko pata nahi hota ki unhone abhi kya kiya hai. Wo isey dekh nahi sakte, lekin unhone yeh kiya hota hai. Aur isliye hamein unhe us jagah tak lana hota hai jahan wo samajh sakein ki unhone abhi kya kiya hai, taaki wo isey dobara (redo) kar sakein. Yahi sikhana (teaching) hai."**

Candida Alvarez, artist, School of the Art Institute of Chicago

Apni research ke shuruati dino mein, spring 2010 mein, maine Savannah College of Art and Design (SCAD) ke foundations professors ke ek group ke sath lunch kiya. Maine table par sabse poocha: "Kya aapko lagta hai ki aap students ko creative banna sikha rahe hain?"

Betty ne sabse pehle jawab diya: "Yeh is baat par nirbhar (depends) karta hai ki aap creativity kise mante hain, kyunki mujhe toh iski definition (tareef) bhi theek se nahi pata, haina?" Wo hasi aur phir boli: "Main unhe dekhna (how to see) sikha rahi hoon."

Linda ne bhi beech mein kaha (chimed in): "Main bhi yahi kehne wali thi."

Betty: "Dekhna kaise hai (How to see). Yeh sochne ka hi ek roop hai, lekin iske bahut se tareeqe hain. Main abhi turant aese saat (7) alag-alag tareeqe soch sakti hoon kisi cheez ko dekhne ke jin par hum kaam karte hain."

Sabne haan mein badbadate (murmured) hue sehmati jatai.

Betty: "Jaise-jaise aap aur zyada draw karna seekhte hain, aapko aur tajurba (experience) milta hai, yeh ek sath (simultaneously) hone lagta hai. Buniyaadi taur par, aap jo dekh rahe hain uske aadhar par. Main kisi cheez ko ek filter ke zariye dekhne, aap jo dekhte hain use samajhne (interpreting) ki baat kar rahi hoon. Mere assignments unke cheezon ko dekhne ke tareeqe ko badal dete hain. Yeh unhe cheezon ko alag dhang se dekhne ki kabiliyat (ability) de raha hai. Mere liye, iska poora matlab yahi hai (that's what it's all about)."

Hamein pakka nahi pata ki creativity kya hai. Lekin ek baar jab aap dekhna seekh jate hain, toh creativity apne aap piche chali aati hai (follows).

### **The Cliché Breast (Ghisi-Piti Chhaati)**

Washington University in St. Louis (WashU) mein, main Bixby Hall mein sculptor Ron Leax ka interview le raha tha. Unhone mujhe ek cigarette ke liye apne sath bahar chalne ko kaha. Jaise hi hum hall se piche ke darwaze ki taraf gaye, hum aisi deewaron ke paas se guzre jo student ke kaam se dhaki hui thin. Leax ne mujhe samjhaya ki yeh ek first-year illustration class ke sketches the. Ek art aur design school mein, lagbhag har deewar par student ka kaam temporary (kuch waqt ke liye) taur par tape se lagaya jata hai (dekhein figure 5.1). Yeh koi formal ya official exhibition nahi hai; yeh rozana ki practice ka hissa hai. In drafts ko kuch dino ya hafton baad hata diya jata hai. Jaise hi hum ek aurat ki chhaati (breast) ke sketch ke paas se guzarte hain, Leax comment karne ke liye rukte hain. Sketch ki taraf ishara karte hue wo kehte hain, "Yeh yahan thoda cliché (ghisa-pita) hai." Main hasta hoon aur kehta hoon, "Kya koi breast kabhi non-cliché bhi ho sakti hai?" Wo hase nahi; maine saaf taur par unki baat galat samjhi thi. Unhone kaha, "Khair, main flesh (maas) aur gravity (gurutvakarshan) ki geography ki samajh ke baare mein soch raha tha." Mujhe koi idea nahi tha ki wo kis baare mein baat kar rahe the. Jis student ne isey sketch kiya tha, bilkul usi ki tarah, mujhe bhi dekhna nahi aata tha.

**FIGURE 5.1** Washington University in St. Louis ka ek hallway jisme student sketches hain. 2015.

Kuch mahinon baad, maine yeh kahani Leax ke WashU colleague Arny Nadler ko sunayi, jo ek sculptor hain aur jinhone paanch saal tak undergraduate art program ke chair ke taur par kaam kiya. Nadler ne kaha, "Mujhe theek se pata hai ki wo kis baare mein baat kar rahe the. Wo students cheezon ko ab kabhi pehle ki tarah nahi dekhenge. Aur main sach mein, main sach mein is baat par yakeen karta hoon. Main sach mein is baat par yakeen karta hoon." Unhone apni teaching ke baare mein bhi yahi kaha: wo students ko "cheezon ko dekhne ke naye tareeqe ijad karne (inventing) aur us asli point (kernel) ko pehchan-ne mein guide karte hain jo sach mein dilchasp hai."

Student ki banayi breast cliché (ghisi-piti) thi. Leax hairan nahi the; shuruaat karne wale students aksar aesa kaam banate hain jo cliché hota hai. Unhe nahi pata hota ki yeh cliché hai. Dekhne ki kabiliyat ke bina, aap nahi jaan sakte ki kya cliché hai aur kya nahi. Jaisa ki School of the Art Institute of Chicago (SAIC) ki Candida Alvarez ne kaha, "Unhe us jagah tak pahunchne ke liye clichés se guzarna padta hai jahan unhe hona chahiye. Aap unhe clichés tak jane se nahi rok sakte, kyunki unhe tab tak pata hi nahi hota ki yeh cliché hai jab tak wo us tak pahunchte nahi."

**Ek baar jab aap isey dekh lete hain, toh aap isey dekhna band nahi kar sakte.**

#### Haven Lin-Kirk, University of Southern California

Cliché breast ki kahani mere dimaag mein atak gayi (stuck with me). Saalon baad, maine isey Beth Grabowski ke samne rakha, jo ek artist hain aur University of North Carolina in Chapel Hill (UNC) mein padhati hain. Nadler ki tarah, wo turant samajh gayin: "Mujhe theek se pata hai ki wo kis baare mein baat kar rahe hain. Iska talluq dekhna seekhne se hai. Unhone us breast ko, us insaan ko dekhne ke bajaye, unke dimaag mein ek symbol (nishani) tha ki ek breast kaisa hota hai aur unhone dekhne ke bajaye wahi draw kar diya."

SAIC ki Claudia Hart virtual reality aur high-end technologies ke sath kaam karti hain. Jab humne dekhna seekhne ke baare mein baat ki, toh unhone ise *deep breathing (gehri saans lena)* kaha. "Visual language (dekhne wali bhasha) unke analytical mind (jaanch karne wale dimaag) ke sath jis tarah kaam karti hai wo aisi cheez nahi hai jo qudrati taur par (naturally) aati ho. Yeh ek physical, bodily experience (jismani tajurba) hai, dekhna, samajhna (perceiving), aur mehsoos karna. Main unhe ise deconstruct (tod kar samajhna) karna sikhati hoon." Aap isey seedhe taur par (directly) nahi sikha sakte; agar aap students ko yeh batane ki koshish karte hain ki dekhna kaise hai, toh wo nahi samjhenge. Hart ne kaha:

*Unme se kuch kehte hain "Mujhe samajh nahi aa raha aap kya keh rahi hain." Aur main kehti hoon, "Fikar mat karo. Tum samajh jaoge."*

School of Visual Arts (SVA) ke ek photographer Accra Shepp ka bhi yahi tajurba (experience) raha hai: "Kabhi-kabhi main student ko instruct karunga (hidayat dunga), shayad student ko order bhi dunga, ki wo isey ek khas tareeqe se kare jo mujhe lagta hai ki kaam ko behtar support karega. Agar student mujhse behas (fighting) kar raha hai, toh main kehta hoon, main samajhta hoon. Lekin tum abhi tak isey nahi dekh pa rahe ho. Isliye main tumse isey dono tareeqon se karne ko kahunga aur hum classroom mein is par nazar dalenge."

Dekhna seekhna ek niji badlaav (personal transformation) hai. Yeh asaan nahi hai. Professor aapko bata sakta hai ki kahan dekhna hai; professor aapko bata sakta hai ki kya dhoondhna hai; lekin aap phir bhi isey nahi dekh payenge. Batana (Telling) kaam nahi karta. Iske bajaye, professors students ko ek dhyan se design kiye gaye raste (pathway) se guide karte hain. Raste ke aakhir mein, students dekhna seekh chuke hote hain.

Cheryl Wassenaar ki WashU mein incoming first-year students ke liye course mein, wo unhe dusre hafte mein yeh assignment deti hain:

*Main students se black construction paper se pachaas (50) shapes katne ko kehti hoon. Ek 3 × 3 ya 5 × 5 inch square par pachaas shapes. Maqsad yeh hai ki sabse baarik nuances (fark), contour lines aur edge quality par charcha (discussion) shuru karne ke liye saikdon shapes hon. Hum ek shape ko dekhte hain aur turant isey kisi aisi cheez se jodne ke baare mein sochne lagte hain jise hum pehle se jante hain. Hum yeh sochne lagte hain ki wo shape kya ho sakti hai. Bhale hi yeh sirf black construction paper hai, iski density (ghanapan) aur wazan (weight) aur hilne ki kabiliyat ya na-kabiliyat ke baare mein sochein. Kya yeh kisi aisi cheez jaisa dikhne lagta hai jise hum pehchante hain? Toh hum bahut saare (shapes) lagate hain aur is ke baare mein baat karna shuru karte hain. Main kehti hoon, "In cheezon ko adjectives (visheshan/khasiyat batane wale shabdon) ke sath bayan (describe) karein." Pehle hum bas unhe shabdon ke sath bayan karna shuru karte hain. Yeh bas ek dhabbe (blob) jaisa lag sakta hai. Lekin wo shapes ko bayan karne ke ek jaise tareeqe dhoondhne lagte hain. Aur phir main ek detective ki tarah poochne lagti hoon, "Aesa kyun hai? Tum aisa kyun kehte ho? Wo cheez bhari (heavy) kyun lag rahi hai? Wo dusri sust (sluggish) kyun lag rahi hai?" Iska talluq shayad niche ki taraf curve ke sabse chote hisse se ho sakta hai, aur hum ek tarah ke thehrav (settling), ya wazan ka andaza (infer) lagate hain. Agar koi cheez thodi si uthi hui hai, ya agar koi cheez niche square ke kinare par baithi hai, toh ek shape aisa lag sakta hai jaise wo kisi bhari cheez se bana ho aur dusra tairta hua (buoyant) lag sakta hai.*

Gaur karein ki students ko construction paper ka istemal karna hota hai, kisi computer program ka nahi. Maine unse poocha kyun, aur unhone isey is tarah samjhaya: "Agar aap cheezon ko physically idhar-udhar hila sakte hain, toh yeh behtar hai. Learning ke beech kinetic connections (harkat se jude rishton) ke baare mein studies hui hain. Agar aap physically apne hathon aur motor skills ko shamil (involved) kar lete hain, toh ek alag hi tarah ki learning hoti hai. Yeh unhe mouse par atke rehne ki tulna mein zyada wasee (broader) tareeqe se train karta hai. Phir wo badlaav (changes), aur options (vikalpon) ko nahi dekh rahe hote hain. Agar aap cheezon ko physically idhar-udhar nahi hila rahe hain, toh aap bahut jaldi sikud (narrow) jate hain aur aap bahut jaldi tay kar lete hain ki 'ise aisa hi hona chahiye.'"

Pehla assignment unhe dekhna seekhne ke raste par chalata hai. Wo is project ke sath is safar (journey) ko aage badhati hain:

*Dusra project jo main unke sath karti hoon, uska goal unhe ek idea par variations (badlaav) dekhne mein madad karna hai. Main unse do images chun-ne ko kehti hoon. Mujhe isse koi farq nahi padta ki wo kya hain. Main unhe 10 shabd deti hoon, jinme "aggressive (gusse wala)," "heavy (bhari)," "shy (sharmila)," "still (shant/ruka hua)" shamil hain. Phir, unhe ek page par 10 alag-alag arrangements (tarteeb) mein lagayein. Agar aap chahein toh unka scale (size) badal sakte hain. Wo dekhenge ki aap sirf arrangement ke sath poori tarah se alag ideas communicate kar sakte hain. Yeh ek zyada conceptual (khayali) cheez se ek zyada physical (thos) cheez mein badal jata hai. Wo is baare mein sach mein nahi sochte jab tak ki aap ise point out (ishara) na karein. Jaise hi aap isey point out karte hain, wo kehte hain, "Oh, okay, main yeh dekh sakta hoon." Lekin unhe ise describe (bayan) karna mushkil lagta hai. Yeh kahin zyada asardaar (effective) hota hai agar aap unse puchein ki wo jo dekh rahe hain use explain karein aur aapko describe karein, isse pehle ki aap unhe apni hypothesis (andaza) batayein.*

Maine unse poocha, assignment par kaam shuru karne se pehle unhe ek lecture kyun nahi dete? Kya aap unhe nahi bata sakti ki arrangements concepts ko kaise communicate kar sakte hain? Shayad aap unhe dikha sakti hain ki isey "gussa (anger)" jaise kisi ek shabd ke sath kaise karna hai, taaki wo shuruat kar sakein? Wo is baare mein bahut clear thin ki wo is tarah kyun nahi padhati hain:

*Agar main bas unhe yeh karne ko keh doon, toh isme discovery (khoj) ka koi ehsaas nahi hoga. Wo samajh jayenge, lekin wo shayad "anger" ko usi tareeqe se samjhenge jaise maine anger ko bayan kiya tha. Iska ek maqsad (point) is baat ke hamare visual (dekhne wale) ideas ko badhana (extend) hai ki anger kaisa dikh sakta hai. Anger kaisa dikh sakta hai iske baare mein bahut stereotypical (roodhiwadi/aam) ideas hote hain. Lekin agar wo dusre tareeqe dhoondh saken, ek naye (inventive) tareeqe se, ek thode naye tareeqe se, anger kaisa dikhta hai aur kaisa mehsoos hota hai (jiske baare mein hum pehle se jante hain) iski kuch maujuda connotations (mayne) ko sath lekar, aur isey hamein aese tareeqe se dikhayein jo kuch hadd tak an-socha (unexpected) ho, toh unhone seekh liya hai ki apni soch mein inventive (naya) kaise banna hai, visual stereotypes aur clichés se aage kaise jana hai.*

Jab wo yeh khud karte hain, "Toh ek discovery (khoj) hoti hai. Wo isey aakhiri nateeje (end conclusion) par seedhe jump karne ke bajaye, raste mein (along the way) samajhte hain." Unka intended learning outcome (seekhne ka nateeja) kya hai? "Page par maujood har cheez forces ka ek juda hua jaal (connected web) hai jo sab aapas mein interact karte hain, aur agar aap ek ko hilate hain, toh aapne poore arrangement ko asar (affected) dala hai." Jab aap yeh padh rahe honge, toh aap soch rahe honge, yeh toh simple aur saaf (obvious) lagta hai. Beshak har cheez judi hui hai! Lekin yeh mumkin hai ki aapki samajh visual (dekhne wali) ke bajaye intellectual (dimaagi) ho; aur yeh aapko dekhne aur create karne ke liye taiyar nahi karta. Wassenaar kahengi, nahi, aap sach mein nahi samajhte ki visual connectedness ko kaise dekhna hai jab tak ki aap kaam na karein.

Richard Mehl 2002 se SVA mein padha rahe hain. Unhone mujhe apni teaching ke shuruati saalon ki ek kahani sunayi. Unke paas ek yaadgaar (memorable) continuing ed (aage ki padhai wali) student thi: ek lawyer jo pehli baar art class le rahi thi. Wo apni mid-forties (45 saal ke aas-paas) mein thi. "Class ke aakhir mein, usne kaha, 'Maine yeh samajh liya (figured out) ki cheezon ko waise kaise dekhna hai jaise aap unhe dekhte hain.' Mujhe ehsaas hua, oh wow, toh main unhe yahi sikha raha hoon, dekhna kaise hai. Us student ne bilkul sahi pakda (nailed it)." Mehl ne students ko dekhna sikhana seekh liya tha, lekin unhe yeh ehsaas nahi hua tha ki wo yahi kar rahe the. Uske baad ke saalo mein, unhone lagatar ek aesi pedagogy (padhane ka tareeqa) develop ki hai jo students ko alag dhang se dekhne mein madad karti hai.

*Dekhna seekhne se mera yahi matlab hai. Aap judgmental (faisla karne wala) banna seekh rahe hain, yeh samajhne ke liye ki aapne in design principles ke lihaz se kya banaya hai. Mujhe yeh kyun pasand hai? Chalo is baare mein sochte hain. Kya chal रहा hai? Wo isey practice ke zariye, cheezon ko karne aur iske baare mein baat karne ke process ke zariye seekhte hain. Wo cheezon ko samajhna shuru karte hain. Wo yeh samajhna shuru karte hain ki wo jo kar rahe hain use kaise dekhna hai. Der-saber (Sooner or later), yeh poori tarah se aadat (intuitive) ban jata hai, aur hamein is ke baare mein aur baat nahi karni padti, kyunki wo bas wahi dekh rahe hote hain jo main dekhta hoon. Yahi sab kuch hai. Main hamesha is idea par wapas aata hoon—aankhein kholna, seekhna, dekhna, dekhna sikhana. Dekhna sach mein important ho jata hai. Aapko lagta hai ki aap jante hain ki aapne kya kiya hai; koi aur isey dekhta hai aur kuch poori tarah alag (completely different) kehta hai. Students is baat se hamesha hairan (surprised) hote hain.*

### **Jab Yeh Kaam Kar Raha Hota Hai (When It's Working)**

**"Main aakar yeh nahi kehta, 'Yeh ache hain, yeh kachra (crap) hain.' Main kehta hoon, 'Kya kaam kar raha hai (working) aur kya kaam nahi kar raha hai? Inmein se kaun sa aesa kuch kar raha hai jo tumhe dilchasp lag raha hai?'"**

Arny Nadler, Washington University in St. Louis

Jab students se poocha jata hai ki wo kisi sathi (peer) ke kaam ke baare mein kya sochte hain, toh wo aksar "Mujhe yeh pasand hai (I like it)" aur "yeh acha hai (it's good)" par wapas aa jate hain. Yeh dekhna seekhne ke raste mein aata hai, kyunki dekhne ke kabil hona yeh seekhne ke baare mein nahi hai ki aapko kya pasand hai aur kya nahi. Professors yeh kehne se bachne ki koshish karte hain ki student ka kaam "acha (good)" hai, aur wo shayad hi kabhi "Mujhe yeh pasand hai" kehte hain (bhale hi unhe yeh pasand *ho*). Wo students ko bhi aisa kehne nahi dete. SVA mein ek painter, Steve DeFrank, khari baatein karte (blunt) hain: "Main students ko 'Mujhe pasand hai' shabdon ka istemal nahi karne deta. Main kehta hoon, 'Mujhe nahi pata iska kya matlab hai.'" Professor ko yeh pasand hai ya nahi, yeh be-matlab (irrelevant) hai. Ek kaam hamesha progress (banne ke amal) mein hota hai, hamesha move kar raha hota hai, hamesha arzi (tentative) hota hai. Isey "acha" kehne ka matlab (implies) hai ki yeh ruka hua (static) aur poora (complete) hai. Iska matlab hai ki student ka kaam khatam ho gaya hai. Iske bajaye, professors aese shabdon ka istemal karte hain jo kaam ko dynamic (harkat mein), process mein, aage badhne ke liye taiyar (poised to move forward) ke taur par describe karte hain. Kaam student se alag ek wajood (separate being) hai aur iski apni khasiyate (qualities) hain. Iske apne ideas hote hain ki yeh kahan ja raha hai.

5 <br>ie Sa re



**FIGURE 5.2** Ruben Salinas. *Nahimana*. Paper par Black tempera. 18 x 24 inches. 2014.

Toh jab aap kisi student ke kaam ka review kar rahe hote hain toh aap kya kehte hain? Aap unhe kaise batate hain ki wo sahi raste (right track) par hain? Unke process mein aage badhne mein madad karne ke liye aap unhe kya advice de sakte hain? Maine lagatar professors ko, har discipline mein, yeh kehte hue suna "Yeh kaam kar raha hai (It's working)" jab kisi student ka kaam . . . kya, exactly? Main bata sakta tha ki art aur design mein *working* ek zaroori (essential) concept tha, aur yeh dekh pane ki kabiliyat hona ki koi cheez kab kaam kar rahi hai, ek ahem (key) learning outcome (seekhne ka nateeja) hai. Lekin mujhe samajh nahi aaya ki iska kya matlab tha. Isliye maine seedhe taur par poocha: "Kisi cheez ko 'working (kaam kar raha hai)' kehne ka kya matlab hai?" Professors ko ise samjhane mein kafi mushkil (trouble) hui; ya shayad mujhe hi unke explanations ko samajhne mein mushkil hui. "Working" ek aesa shabd hai jise sabhi professors samajhte hain, lekin wo theek se nahi bata sakte ki iska kya matlab hai. Mujhe ehsaas hua ki aap isey tab tak nahi samajh sakte jab tak ki aap dekhna na seekh lein. Jab aap dekhna jante hain, toh aap "working" aur "not working" ke beech ka contrast (farq) dekh sakte hain.

"Working" kaam mein aage ki possibilities (sambhavnaon) ki taraf ishara karta hai. Jab yeh kaam kar raha hota hai, toh isme potential (dam/kabiliyat) hota hai. Julie Varland (architecture, SCAD) ne ek student se kaha ki unka kaam "sambhavit taur par (potentially) ek bahut hi exciting (romanchak) disha (direction) mein ja raha hai." Patricia Olynyk (artist, WashU) ne kaha ki jab koi cheez kaam kar rahi hoti hai, toh yeh "aur zyada complex sawalon ki ek badi tadad (multiplicity) khol deti hai."

Ya wo kahenge ki ek kaam *dilchasp (interesting)* hai, jiska matlab yeh bhi hai ki is par aage kaam karna (pursuing) qabil-e-gaur hai. Maine illustrator Ruben Salinas dwara padhayi jane wali SCAD ki ek foundations class visit ki. Students tarah-tarah ke draft sketches laye the, asal mein sirf possibilities, jinhe wo "thumbnails" kehte the. Unka agla task ek ko chunna aur usi ke sath process ko aage badhana tha. Ek point par, unhone apne students se poocha, "Aapke liye sabse interesting kaun sa hai? Main khud se yahi poochta." Wassenaar ne students ko yeh sikhane ke baare mein baat ki ki "kin ideas ko pursue (aage le jana) karna interesting hai, aur kinhe nahi."

Studio critiques mein dusre aam shabd *successful (kamyaab)*, *viable (mumkin)*, aur *effective (asardaar)* hain. Yeh sab *working* ke variants (roop) hain. Ek painting ya sculpture "not working" ya gair-asardaar (ineffective) kaise ho sakta hai? Yeh tasavvur (imagine) karna mushkil nahi hai ki ek advertisement asardaar ho sakta hai; SCAD ke ek advertising professor ne ad ki effectiveness (asardari) ki ehmiyat par zor diya. WashU mein communication design mein Douglas Dowd ne bhi kaha, "aapko mere liye kuch aesa banana hoga jo mazboot (strong) aur asardaar (effective) ho." Advertising aur communication design dono hi dekhne wale (viewer) ko prabhavit (influence) karne ki koshish kar rahe hain. Mujhe lagta tha ki ek painting kuch *karne (do)* ki koshish nahi kar rahi hoti. Main galat tha.

Nadler ne mujhe bataya: "Artists aur designers cheezon ko dekhne aur gaur karne (look at) ke naye tareeqe ijad kar rahe hain. Nayi visual possibilities pesh kar rahe hain jinhe dekhne ke log aadi (used to) nahi hain." Maine unse poocha, aap students ko yeh dekhna kaise sikhate hain ki unki kin cheezon mein potential hai?

*Main kahunga, "Okay. Kya kaam kar raha hai aur kya kaam nahi kar raha hai?" Ya main kahunga, "Inmein se aapko kis cheez mein dilchaspi (interests) hai aur kyun? Inmein se kaun sa aesa kuch kar raha hai jo aapko dilchasp lagta hai?" Aapko bas unke liye aisa karne ke experiences (tajurbe) set up karne hote hain. Mera kaam aise visual connections banana hai jo wo nahi bana pa rahe hain. Main aise nuances (baarikiyan) dekh pata hoon jo shayad wo shuru mein (right off the bat) notice nahi kar paate. Main aisi possibilities dekh pata hoon jinhe dekhne ke qabil abhi wo puri tarah nahi hain.*

Jab painter Stephen Knudsen SCAD mein apne students ke sath famous painters par charcha (discuss) karte hain, toh wo students ko unki paintings ko ek naye tareeqe se dekhne mein guide karte hain. Unhone mujhe ek misaal di ki wo apne students ke sath kya karte hain: "Kya ho agar hum ek Rothko ko lein aur use tod dein (de-assimilated)? Kya farq hoga? Hum cheezon ko kat-te hain (dissect) aur unhe barbad (ruin) karte hain taaki dekh sakein ki wo kaise kaam kar rahe hain." WashU mein ek painter, Lisa Bulawsky bhi kehti hain ki wo students ko yeh dekhna sikhati hain ki "kaam kaise banaya gaya tha ya kaam kyun banaya gaya tha." WashU ki ek aur painter, Patricia Olynyk ne mujhe bataya ki wo students se dusre artworks ko dekhne aur poochne ke liye kehti hain: "Wo kaun se mechanisms (tareeqe) hain jinke zariye yeh artworks kaam karte (work) hain?" Yeh intentionality (irade) aur decisions (faisle) kaise liye jate hain yeh samajhne ke baare mein hai. SCAD ke advertising professor lagatar students se poochte hain, "Isey is dusre tareeqe se kyun nahi kiya gaya? Kya yeh aur bura hota, aur kyun?"

### **Idea aur Reality ke Beech ka Disconnect (Fasla)**

**Kabhi-kabhi idea aur unhone jo asal mein banaya hai (actually made) uske beech ek bahut bada disconnect (fasla) hota hai. Toh use wazeh (articulating) karna definitely process ka hissa hai.**

Amy Honchell, painter aur sculptor, School of Visual Arts

Dekhna seekhna aasaan nahi hai. SCAD ki ek architect, Julie Varland ne kaha: "Yeh Mount Everest ki unchai wali ek khayi (cliff) hai." Baad mein, unki studio class ko observe karte waqt, maine unhe ek critique ke dauran ek student se yeh kehte hue suna: "Aap aisi bahut si cheezon ke baare mein baat kar rahe hain jo asal drawing mein nahi hain." Yeh wahi *mismatch* (mel na khana) hai: jab kaam ke liye student ka irada (intention) us cheez se match nahi karta jo student ne banayi (generated) hai. Isse pehle ki students dekh sakein, wo yeh dekhne ke qabil nahi hote ki wahan koi mismatch hai. Wo apne kaam ko dekhte hain aur unhe lagta hai ki yeh unke irade ko darshata (reflects) hai. Studio critiques ka ek zaroori goal (maqsad) students ko in mismatches ko dekhne mein guide karna hai. Zyada-tar professors ka kehna hai ki isme kam se kam do saal lagte hain.

**FIGURE 5.3** Julie Varland critique, architecture, SCAD. 2010.

Jab students apna draft deewar par lagate hain, aur class ko yeh batate hue critique shuru karte hain ki unhone kya kiya hai aur kyun, toh professor jaldi hi samajh jate (realizes) hain ki wo jo keh rahe hain wo deewar par lage kaam se match nahi kha raha. Jab aesa hota hai, toh aap asani se student se keh sakte hain, "tum jo keh rahe ho wo main nahi dekh raha hoon," aur professors aksar aesa karte hain. Lekin wo jante hain ki unhe batana kafi nahi hai. Students tab tak sach mein nahi samjhenge jab tak ki unhe ek process se guide na kiya jaye.

Steve DeFrank (SVA) ne mujhe yeh kahani sunayi jo dikhati hai ki cliché kaam kis tarah idea aur reality ke beech ek mismatch ka nateeja (results) deta hai:

*Kuch saal pehle ek student, khuda bhalayi kare jawan logo ki (God bless youth), is jawan aurat ne chhote bacchon (tiny babies), gudiya jaisi cheezon ki casts (sanche) banaye. Aur phir unhe nakli khoon (fake blood) mein duboya aur chakuon (knives) ke sath latka diya. Kafi khooni, kafi aam (generic), lekin inmein se kuch bacchon ke liye, yeh pehli baar koi statement dene ki koshish (trying to make a statement) hoti hai. Aur critique mein, sabhi students ne kaha, aisa lagta hai ki yeh abortion (hamal girane) ke baare mein hai. Student bas sharm se laal (bright red) ho rahi thi. Jab uske bolne ki baari aayi, toh usne kaha, iska abortion se koi lena-dena nahi hai. Mujhe yakeen nahi ho raha ki aap log yeh dekh rahe hain. Aur hamara reaction tha, sach mein (really)? Agar tum yeh nahi dekh sakin, toh tum ek tarah se andhi (blind) ho! Aur saalon baad, jab wo ek senior thi, toh wo mere paas aayi aur boli, My God, Steve, mujhe yakeen nahi hota ki maine yeh nahi socha ki yeh abortion ke baare mein tha. Maine kaha, kabhi-kabhi hum jo banate hain uske liye hum andhe (blind) hote hain. Kabhi-kabhi hamein nahi pata hota ki hum kya banate hain, aur saalon baad, hamein pata chalta hai.*

Maine kuch hafton baad DeFrank ko email kiya, jab main hamare interview ko transcribe kar raha tha, yeh poochne ke liye ki us student ne kya *socha* tha ki usne kya kiya hai. "Usne mujhe bataya ki wo kuch alarming (darawna/chaukane wala) karna chahti thi. Toh usne kuch shocking aur disturbing banane ki koshish ki. Bloody babies (khooni bacche), ek naye artist ke liye, bahut cutting edge (naya/advance) the. Agar har bloody baby sculpture aur cigarette butts wali Jack Daniels ki bottle ke liye mujhe ek nickel (sikka) milta, toh main ek ameer aadmi hota!" Art aur design padhane wale har insaan ko aesa hi experience hota hai. "Yeh sach mein mazakiya (funny) hai. Sculpture faculty ka ek lagatar chalne wala mazaak tha un saari cheezon ke baare mein jinhe hum unke hadd se zyada istemal (overuse) ki wajah se ban karna chahte the. Bloody babies sabse upar the, usi tarah cigarette butts, beer ya alcohol ki bottles, aur bloody Jackson Pollock marks bhi. Mujhe lagta hai ki art school ke professors ek sath aayen toh hamein ehsaas hoga ki hum sabki aesi hi (similar) kahaniyan hain."

**Students kabhi-kabhi ek mamuli (mediocre) formal idea ke upar ek uncha (lofty) concept rakh dete hain. Jab students concept ko pehle rakhte hain, toh hum insaan par insaan ke zulam (man's inhumanity) ke baare mein lamba-chauda (endlessly) bol sakte hain, aur phir hum kabhi bhi deewar par lage us object (cheez) ke baare mein sach mein baat nahi karte.**

Steve DeFrank, SVA

DeFrank ne students ko apne mismatches ko aur jaldi dekhne mein madad karne ke liye ek hal (solution) nikala: "Main students ko shuru mein bolne nahi deta kyunki wo batane (communicating) mein bahut ache hote hain. Maine bahut baar dekha hai ki koi insaan par insaan ke zulam (man's inhumanity to man) ke baare mein baat kar raha hota hai aur mujhe wo nahi dikhta. Mujhe bas paper par colored pencil dikhti hai. Agar main artist ko bolne doon, toh baki sabhi students kahenge, oh haan, main wo dekh sakta hoon. Aur main soch raha hota hoon nahi, tum wo bilkul nahi dekh rahe ho. Toh main shuru mein student ko bolne nahi deta, aur phir jab wo apna lamba chauda bhashan (spiel) dete hain, toh baaki sabhi students, wo kehte hain yeh bakwas hai (call bullshit), yeh waisa nahi lag raha jise hum dekh rahe hain." SVA ke photographer Accra Shepp ne bhi isi experience ko dohrata (echoed):

*Jab students pehli baar kaam ke baare mein baat karna shuru karte hain, oh my gosh, wo in cheezon mein kood padte (launch into) hain. Yeh sab abstract (khayali) baatein hoti hain. Aur main kehta hoon, "Kya tum picture ki taraf ishara (point) karke mujhe bata sakte ho ki connection kahan hai? Yeh physically (zaahiri taur par) kahan ho रहा hai?" Yahan tak ki jo log conceptually (ideas par) kaam karte hain unhe bhi yeh jan-na zaroori hai. Image ko hi asal message dena hoga (carry the water) aur isse bachne ka koi rasta nahi hai.*

*Students soch sakte hain ki wo ek cheez kar rahe hain lekin wo sach mein kuch aur hi kar rahe hote hain. Khud ko dhokha dena (Self-deception) kafi aam (frequent) hai. Toh main unhe sun raha hota hoon aur hamesha un par vishwas nahi kar raha hota. Kabhi-kabhi wo mujhse bas saaf jhooth (lie outright) bolte hain aur mujhe kuch sach mein bewaqoofi (stupid) bhari baat batate hain. Main jawab deta hoon, khair, yeh kaam mein toh nahi hai, jo main dekhta hoon wo yeh hai. Aur phir wo jhenpte hue (sheepishly) muskurate hain kyunki wo jante hain ki wo jhooth bol rahe the.*

Catalina Freixas ek architect hain jo WashU mein first-year students ko padhati hain. Unka bhi ek aesa hi (similar) tajurba (experience) hai. "Kabhi-kabhi unhe lagta hai ki wo jo kar rahe hain wo wo nahi hota jo wo bana rahe hote hain. Sochna (Thinking) aur banana (making) ek sath nahi aate, aur yeh ek problem hai, kyunki jab wo bayan (express) karte hain ki wo kya soch rahe hain, toh wo (kaam mein) nazar nahi aata (doesn't come through)." Ek desk crit (desk par jaize) mein, wo student ke sath baithti hain, aur "hum dono us project ko samajhne aur streamline (seedha/asaan) karke kuch aesa banane ki koshish kar rahe hote hain jisme unke sochne aur banane ke beech tal-mel (coherent) ho." Desk crits ke alawa har student ke sath unki ek-ghante ki private meetings hoti hain. Un meetings mein, "Main yeh pakka (make sure) karti hoon ki unhe pata ho ki unke khali-pan (voids) ya disconnects (faslay) kahan ho rahe hain. Agar koi aisi cheez hai jahan unki drawings unki baton se align (mel nahi kha rahi) nahi hain, ya unki drawing mein us baat ko support karne ke liye kisi cheez ki kami hai jo wo keh rahe hain, toh main unse wapas jane aur usay apne design mein dobara shamil (retrofit) karne ko kehti hoon."

Jab Freixas students ke sath unke idea ko unke kaam ke sath align (milane) karne mein madad karne ke liye kaam karti hain, toh wo isey *streamlining* kehti hain. Hamare interview ke baad, maine Freixas ki studio class visit ki (dekhein figure 5.4). Maine unhe ek student se yeh kehte hue suna: "Main chahti hoon ki tum yeh samjho ki jab tum represent (dikha) kar rahe ho, jab tum koi statement (dawa) dete ho, toh isey wahan hona chahiye. Warna, isme koi dam (no teeth) nahi hai. Tum jo baatein kar rahe ho uske hisab se is waqt tumhari drawings bahut, bahut romantic (khayali) hain."

**FIGURE 5.4**

Catalina Freixas critique, architecture, Washington University in St. Louis. Freixas deewar par lage student ke kaam ki taraf ishara kar rahi hain; student unke piche chhipa hua hai. Samne (foreground) baithe students ka class mein baad mein apna critique hoga. 2011.

Mismatch (mel na khana) zaroori nahi ki koi problem ho. Iska matlab yeh nahi hai ki student ne koi sharminda karne wali galti (embarrassing mistake) ki hai. Iska matlab yeh nahi hai ki student ek bura architect hai. Yahan tak ki professional artists aur designers bhi aisi cheezein banate hain jo unhe hairan (surprise) karti hain, kuch aisa jo waisa nahi hota jaisa unhone socha tha ki wo kar rahe hain. Problem mismatch nahi hai; problem isey na dekh pana hai. Ek mismatch dilchasp (exciting) hota hai kyunki yeh kuch aesa zahir (reveals) karta hai jiski aapko umeed nahi thi. Yeh koi failure (nakaami) nahi hai; yeh iteration (dohraav) ka ek mauka hai. Aap sunte hain ki kaam aapse kya keh raha hai aur aap us naye raste par chalte hain jis par yeh aapko le jata hai. Experienced artists aur designers apne khud ke working process mein mismatches ki umeed (expect) karte hain, aur wo jante hain ki students bhi unka tajurba (experience) karenge. Mismatches ek aise creative process ko aage badhate (drive) hain jahan aap kaam ke sath ek khoj bhare dialogue (exploratory dialogue) mein shamil (engage) hote hain.

Mismatch ko dekh pane ke kabil hona kaam ke sath dialogue ke raste (path) ka pehla qadam (step) hai. Lekin students nahi dekh pate ki wahan koi mismatch hai. Pedagogical (padhane wala) challenge do-tarfa (twofold) hai: Pehla, students ko aisi jagah pahunchna hoga jahan wo mismatch ko dekh sakein. Dusra, students ko mismatch ko ek mauke (opportunity) ke taur par dekhna seekhna hoga, na ki apne irade (intention) ko poora karne mein nakaami ke taur par. Idea aur haqeeqat (reality) ke beech ki kashmakash (tension) process ko aage badhati hai.

Jab students dheere-dheere dekhna seekh rahe hote hain, toh wo in mismatches ko notice (dhyan dena) karne mein behtar ho jate hain. Shuru mein, wo isey ek failure mante hain. Wo kaam ko apne shuruati irade (original intention) ke sath align (milane) karne ke liye modify (badalne) ki koshish karte hain. Yeh instinct (aadat) creativity ke linear model ko pakde rakhne se aati hai: Aap ek idea ke sath shuru karte hain aur phir aap us idea ko execute (pura) karte hain. Lekin jab aap apne irade (intention) ko kaam par thopne (force) ki koshish karte hain, toh aap kaam ko waisa dekhna band kar dete hain jaisa wo hai. Ek kamyaab (successful) creative process mein, aap kaam ke sath ek dialogue mein involve (engage) hote hain. Jab aap idea ko hawi hone (dominate) dete hain, toh aap kaam ko dabane (override), use kabu (behave) mein karne, use wo karne par majboor karne ki koshish karte hain jo aap chahte hain. Art ke baare mein yeh ek aam galatfehmi (misconception) hai: Artist ke paas ek message hota hai aur phir wo us message ko apne medium ke materials mein communicate (bhejne) karne ka kaam karta hai. Lekin jab aap original idea se match karne ke liye kaam ko modify (badalne) ki koshish karte hain, toh iska nateeja gair-asardaar (less effective) creativity hota hai.

Los Angeles mein ek graphic designer Louise Sandhaus, jo California Institute of the Arts (CalArts) mein padhati hain, unke sath ek baatcheet mein humne is baare mein discuss kiya ki kaise students aksar kuch aesa banate (generate) hain jo usse alag hota hai jiske baare mein unhone socha tha ki unhone kiya hai. Wo pehle unhe mismatch ko notice karne ke liye guide karti hain. Phir wo unhe is baat par gaur (consider) karne ke liye guide karti hain ki unhone jo banaya hai uski haqeeqat (reality) unke original idea se behtar ho sakti hai: "Wo jahan pahunche hain wo us nateeje se behtar hai jahan wo soch rahe the ki wo ja rahe hain." Jab students apne kaam ko saaf taur par (clearly) dekh sakte hain, toh wo apne process mein zyada asardaar tareeqe se aage badhte hain.

Behtar ideas materials ke sath ek dialogue se ubhar kar (emerge) aate hain. Instructors students ko naye idea ya problem ko dekhne ke liye guide karte hain jo kaam se ubhar kar aaya hai, aur us nayi problem ya sawal ko pehchan-ne (identify) mein madad karte hain jo kaam ne unke samne pesh kiya hai. Learning goal (seekhne ka maqsad) students ko apne original idea ko kam ehmiyat (deemphasize) dene mein madad karna hai, aur unka dhyan (focus) ideas ko ubharne (emerge) ka mauka dene ke liye process par bharosa (relying) karne ki taraf modna (shift) hai.

Mismatches aksar isliye hote hain kyunki students pehle se hi us aakhiri kaam (final work) ki ek tasveer (internal representation) bana chuke hote hain jiski unhe umeed hoti hai, aur sochte hain ki wo jo final image banane ka irada (intending) kar rahe hain wo kaam mein dikhai de rahi hai. Yahi ek wajah hai ki professors mante hain ki aakhiri nateeje (endpoint) par focus students ko ek kamyaab creative process se guzarne se bhatkata (distracts) hai, aur iska nateeja gair-asardaar (ineffective) kaam hota hai. Sandra Reed, ek SCAD painting professor ne mujhe bataya: "Maine teaching ke saalon se yeh seekha hai ki ek naye (young) artist ko behtareen ideas aayenge aur wo apne dimaag ki aankhon (mind's eye) mein dekh lega ki final painting kaisi dikhti hai. Lekin jaise hi unka samna materials se hota hai, wo kho jate hain. Wo bahut asani se nirash (discouraged) ho sakte hain." Unhone kaha ki unka maqsad students ko "kaam kaisa dikhta hai aur idea kya hai, iske beech ki kashmakash (tension)" ko hal (resolve) karne mein madad karna hai. Students ko aksar "form ko kaam mein lane (make the form work) ke liye un cheezon ko chhodna padta hai jo idea ke lihaz se unke liye bahut ahem (precious) thin."

UNC ki ek printmaker Beth Grabowski ne kaha:

*Yeh ek aam problem hai, ki kaam irade (intention) ke sath align (mel) nahi khata. Kaam kis baare mein hai (what the work is about) aur kaam ko kis cheez ne prerit kiya (what motivated the work) iske beech ka idea. Apne students ke sath, main ek artist ke motivating ideas (prerit karne wale khayalat) aur kaam ka matlab (meaning) kya hai, iske beech ek saaf farq (clear distinction) rakhti hoon. Main kisi student se kabhi nahi poochti, Tum kya karne ki koshish kar rahe the? Main unse poochti hoon, Jab tumne yeh shuru kiya tha toh tum kya soch rahe the. Tumhare motivating ideas kya the? Wo sachai (truth) jiske baare mein main bilkul shuruat se baat karti hoon wo yeh hai ki meaning (matlab), ek capital M ke sath, aisi cheez nahi hai jis par artist ka bahut zyada control hota hai. Us meaning ko dekhne wala (viewer) mukammal (completed) karta hai. Hamesha kuch ajeeb log (outliers) honge, lekin agar log lagatar aapke kaam mein kuch aisa padhte/samajhte (read into) hain jise aap wahan nahi chahte, toh aapko thoda kaam karne ki zaroorat hai.*

### **Baat Karna aur Dekhna (Talking and Seeing)**

**Hum unke ideas ko tod (break down) dete hain aur unhe cheezon ke baare mein sochne ke naye tareeqe dikhate hain.
